 100 namespace detail { 
 101 template <typename Exception> inline void do_throw(const Exception& x) { 
 102   // Silence unreachable code warnings in MSVC and NVCC because these 
 103   // are nearly impossible to fix in a generic code. 
 104   volatile bool b = true; 
 105   if (b) throw x; 
 106 } 
 107 }  // namespace detail 
 108 FMT_END_NAMESPACE 
 109 #      define FMT_THROW(x) detail::do_throw(x) 
 110 #    else 
 111 #      define FMT_THROW(x) throw x 
 112 #    endif 
 113 #  else 
 114 #    define FMT_THROW(x)              \ 
 115       do {                            \ 
 116         static_cast<void>(sizeof(x)); \ 
 117         FMT_ASSERT(false, "");        \ 
 118       } while (false) 
 119 #  endif 
 120 #endif

 174 // __builtin_clz is broken in clang with Microsoft CodeGen: 
 175 // https://github.com/fmtlib/fmt/issues/519 
 176 #if (FMT_GCC_VERSION || FMT_HAS_BUILTIN(__builtin_clz)) && !FMT_MSC_VER 
 177 #  define FMT_BUILTIN_CLZ(n) __builtin_clz(n) 
 178 #endif 
 179 #if (FMT_GCC_VERSION || FMT_HAS_BUILTIN(__builtin_clzll)) && !FMT_MSC_VER 
 180 #  define FMT_BUILTIN_CLZLL(n) __builtin_clzll(n) 
 181 #endif 
 182  
 183 // Some compilers masquerade as both MSVC and GCC-likes or otherwise support 
 184 // __builtin_clz and __builtin_clzll, so only define FMT_BUILTIN_CLZ using the 
 185 // MSVC intrinsics if the clz and clzll builtins are not available. 
 186 #if FMT_MSC_VER && !defined(FMT_BUILTIN_CLZLL) && !defined(_MANAGED) 
 187 #  include <intrin.h>  // _BitScanReverse, _BitScanReverse64 
 188  
 189 FMT_BEGIN_NAMESPACE 
 190 namespace detail { 
 191 // Avoid Clang with Microsoft CodeGen's -Wunknown-pragmas warning. 
 192 #  ifndef __clang__ 
 193 #    pragma intrinsic(_BitScanReverse) 
 194 #  endif 
 195 inline uint32_t clz(uint32_t x) { 
 196   unsigned long r = 0; 
 197   _BitScanReverse(&r, x); 
 198  
 199   FMT_ASSERT(x != 0, ""); 
 200   // Static analysis complains about using uninitialized data 
 201   // "r", but the only way that can happen is if "x" is 0, 
 202   // which the callers guarantee to not happen. 
 203   FMT_SUPPRESS_MSC_WARNING(6102) 
 204   return 31 - r; 
 205 } 
 206 #  define FMT_BUILTIN_CLZ(n) detail::clz(n) 
 207  
 208 #  if defined(_WIN64) && !defined(__clang__) 
 209 #    pragma intrinsic(_BitScanReverse64) 
 210 #  endif 
 211  
 212 inline uint32_t clzll(uint64_t x) { 
 213   unsigned long r = 0; 
 214 #  ifdef _WIN64 
 215   _BitScanReverse64(&r, x); 
 216 #  else 
 217   // Scan the high 32 bits. 
 218   if (_BitScanReverse(&r, static_cast<uint32_t>(x >> 32))) return 63 - (r + 32); 
 219  
 220   // Scan the low 32 bits. 
 221   _BitScanReverse(&r, static_cast<uint32_t>(x)); 
 222 #  endif 
 223  
 224   FMT_ASSERT(x != 0, ""); 
 225   // Static analysis complains about using uninitialized data 
 226   // "r", but the only way that can happen is if "x" is 0, 
 227   // which the callers guarantee to not happen. 
 228   FMT_SUPPRESS_MSC_WARNING(6102) 
 229   return 63 - r; 
 230 } 
 231 #  define FMT_BUILTIN_CLZLL(n) detail::clzll(n) 
 232 }  // namespace detail 
 233 FMT_END_NAMESPACE 
 234 #endif

 244 // An equivalent of `*reinterpret_cast<Dest*>(&source)` that doesn't have 
 245 // undefined behavior (e.g. due to type aliasing). 
 246 // Example: uint64_t d = bit_cast<uint64_t>(2.718); 
 247 template <typename Dest, typename Source> 
 248 inline Dest bit_cast(const Source& source) { 
 249   static_assert(sizeof(Dest) == sizeof(Source), "size mismatch"); 
 250   Dest dest; 
 251   std::memcpy(&dest, &source, sizeof(dest)); 
 252   return dest; 
 253 } 
 254  
 255 inline bool is_big_endian() { 
 256   const auto u = 1u; 
 257   struct bytes { 
 258     char data[sizeof(u)]; 
 259   }; 
 260   return bit_cast<bytes>(u).data[0] == 0; 
 261 } 
 262  
 263 // A fallback implementation of uintptr_t for systems that lack it. 
 264 struct fallback_uintptr { 
 265   unsigned char value[sizeof(void*)]; 
 266  
 267   fallback_uintptr() = default; 
 268   explicit fallback_uintptr(const void* p) { 
 269     *this = bit_cast<fallback_uintptr>(p); 
 270     if (is_big_endian()) { 
 271       for (size_t i = 0, j = sizeof(void*) - 1; i < j; ++i, --j) 
 272         std::swap(value[i], value[j]); 
 273     } 
 274   } 
 275 }; 
 276 #ifdef UINTPTR_MAX 
 277 using uintptr_t = ::uintptr_t; 
 278 inline uintptr_t to_uintptr(const void* p) { return bit_cast<uintptr_t>(p); } 
 279 #else 
 280 using uintptr_t = fallback_uintptr; 
 281 inline fallback_uintptr to_uintptr(const void* p) { 
 282   return fallback_uintptr(p); 
 283 } 
 284 #endif

 286 // Returns the largest possible value for type T. Same as 
 287 // std::numeric_limits<T>::max() but shorter and not affected by the max macro. 
 288 template <typename T> constexpr T max_value() { 
 289   return (std::numeric_limits<T>::max)(); 
 290 } 
 291 template <typename T> constexpr int num_bits() { 
 292   return std::numeric_limits<T>::digits; 
 293 } 
 294 // std::numeric_limits<T>::digits may return 0 for 128-bit ints. 
 295 template <> constexpr int num_bits<int128_t>() { return 128; } 
 296 template <> constexpr int num_bits<uint128_t>() { return 128; } 
 297 template <> constexpr int num_bits<fallback_uintptr>() { 
 298   return static_cast<int>(sizeof(void*) * 
 299                           std::numeric_limits<unsigned char>::digits); 
 300 }

 302 FMT_INLINE void assume(bool condition) { 
 303   (void)condition; 
 304 #if FMT_HAS_BUILTIN(__builtin_assume) 
 305   __builtin_assume(condition); 
 306 #endif 
 307 }

 309 // An approximation of iterator_t for pre-C++20 systems. 
 310 template <typename T> 
 311 using iterator_t = decltype(std::begin(std::declval<T&>())); 
 312 template <typename T> using sentinel_t = decltype(std::end(std::declval<T&>()));

 314 // A workaround for std::string not having mutable data() until C++17. 
 315 template <typename Char> inline Char* get_data(std::basic_string<Char>& s) { 
 316   return &s[0]; 
 317 } 
 318 template <typename Container> 
 319 inline typename Container::value_type* get_data(Container& c) { 
 320   return c.data(); 
 321 }

 323 #if defined(_SECURE_SCL) && _SECURE_SCL 
 324 // Make a checked iterator to avoid MSVC warnings. 
 325 template <typename T> using checked_ptr = stdext::checked_array_iterator<T*>; 
 326 template <typename T> checked_ptr<T> make_checked(T* p, size_t size) { 
 327   return {p, size}; 
 328 } 
 329 #else 
 330 template <typename T> using checked_ptr = T*; 
 331 template <typename T> inline T* make_checked(T* p, size_t) { return p; } 
 332 #endif 
 333  
 334 template <typename Container, FMT_ENABLE_IF(is_contiguous<Container>::value)> 
 335 #if FMT_CLANG_VERSION 
 336 __attribute__((no_sanitize("undefined"))) 
 337 #endif 
 338 inline checked_ptr<typename Container::value_type> 
 339 reserve(std::back_insert_iterator<Container> it, size_t n) { 
 340   Container& c = get_container(it); 
 341   size_t size = c.size(); 
 342   c.resize(size + n); 
 343   return make_checked(get_data(c) + size, n); 
 344 } 
 345  
 346 template <typename T> 
 347 inline buffer_appender<T> reserve(buffer_appender<T> it, size_t n) { 
 348   buffer<T>& buf = get_container(it); 
 349   buf.try_reserve(buf.size() + n); 
 350   return it; 
 351 } 
 352  
 353 template <typename Iterator> inline Iterator& reserve(Iterator& it, size_t) { 
 354   return it; 
 355 } 
 356  
 357 template <typename T, typename OutputIt> 
 358 constexpr T* to_pointer(OutputIt, size_t) { 
 359   return nullptr; 
 360 } 
 361 template <typename T> T* to_pointer(buffer_appender<T> it, size_t n) { 
 362   buffer<T>& buf = get_container(it); 
 363   auto size = buf.size(); 
 364   if (buf.capacity() < size + n) return nullptr; 
 365   buf.try_resize(size + n); 
 366   return buf.data() + size; 
 367 }

 371     std::back_insert_iterator<Container>& it, 
 372     checked_ptr<typename Container::value_type>) { 
 373   return it; 
 374 }

 377 inline Iterator base_iterator(Iterator, Iterator it) { 
 378   return it;

 381 // An output iterator that counts the number of objects written to it and 
 382 // discards them. 
 383 class counting_iterator { 
 384  private: 
 385   size_t count_; 
 386  
 387  public: 
 388   using iterator_category = std::output_iterator_tag; 
 389   using difference_type = std::ptrdiff_t; 
 390   using pointer = void; 
 391   using reference = void; 
 392   using _Unchecked_type = counting_iterator;  // Mark iterator as checked. 
 393  
 394   struct value_type { 
 395     template <typename T> void operator=(const T&) {} 
 396   }; 
 397  
 398   counting_iterator() : count_(0) {} 
 399  
 400   size_t count() const { return count_; } 
 401  
 402   counting_iterator& operator++() { 
 403     ++count_; 
 404     return *this; 
 405   } 
 406  
 407   counting_iterator operator++(int) { 
 408     auto it = *this; 
 409     ++*this; 
 410     return it; 
 411   } 
 412  
 413   value_type operator*() const { return {}; } 
 414 }; 
 415  
 416 template <typename OutputIt> class truncating_iterator_base { 
 417  protected: 
 418   OutputIt out_; 
 419   size_t limit_; 
 420   size_t count_; 
 421  
 422   truncating_iterator_base(OutputIt out, size_t limit) 
 423       : out_(out), limit_(limit), count_(0) {} 
 424  
 425  public: 
 426   using iterator_category = std::output_iterator_tag; 
 427   using value_type = typename std::iterator_traits<OutputIt>::value_type; 
 428   using difference_type = void; 
 429   using pointer = void; 
 430   using reference = void; 
 431   using _Unchecked_type = 
 432       truncating_iterator_base;  // Mark iterator as checked. 
 433  
 434   OutputIt base() const { return out_; } 
 435   size_t count() const { return count_; } 
 436 }; 
 437  
 438 // An output iterator that truncates the output and counts the number of objects 
 439 // written to it. 
 440 template <typename OutputIt, 
 441           typename Enable = typename std::is_void< 
 442               typename std::iterator_traits<OutputIt>::value_type>::type> 
 443 class truncating_iterator; 
 444  
 445 template <typename OutputIt> 
 446 class truncating_iterator<OutputIt, std::false_type> 
 447     : public truncating_iterator_base<OutputIt> { 
 448   mutable typename truncating_iterator_base<OutputIt>::value_type blackhole_; 
 449  
 450  public: 
 451   using value_type = typename truncating_iterator_base<OutputIt>::value_type; 
 452  
 453   truncating_iterator(OutputIt out, size_t limit) 
 454       : truncating_iterator_base<OutputIt>(out, limit) {} 
 455  
 456   truncating_iterator& operator++() { 
 457     if (this->count_++ < this->limit_) ++this->out_; 
 458     return *this; 
 459   } 
 460  
 461   truncating_iterator operator++(int) { 
 462     auto it = *this; 
 463     ++*this; 
 464     return it; 
 465   } 
 466  
 467   value_type& operator*() const { 
 468     return this->count_ < this->limit_ ? *this->out_ : blackhole_; 
 469   } 
 470 }; 
 471  
 472 template <typename OutputIt> 
 473 class truncating_iterator<OutputIt, std::true_type> 
 474     : public truncating_iterator_base<OutputIt> { 
 475  public: 
 476   truncating_iterator(OutputIt out, size_t limit) 
 477       : truncating_iterator_base<OutputIt>(out, limit) {} 
 478  
 479   template <typename T> truncating_iterator& operator=(T val) { 
 480     if (this->count_++ < this->limit_) *this->out_++ = val; 
 481     return *this; 
 482   } 
 483  
 484   truncating_iterator& operator++() { return *this; } 
 485   truncating_iterator& operator++(int) { return *this; } 
 486   truncating_iterator& operator*() { return *this; } 
 487 };

 489 template <typename Char> 
 490 inline size_t count_code_points(basic_string_view<Char> s) { 
 491   return s.size(); 
 492 } 
 493  
 494 // Counts the number of code points in a UTF-8 string. 
 495 inline size_t count_code_points(basic_string_view<char> s) { 
 496   const char* data = s.data(); 
 497   size_t num_code_points = 0; 
 498   for (size_t i = 0, size = s.size(); i != size; ++i) { 
 499     if ((data[i] & 0xc0) != 0x80) ++num_code_points; 
 500   } 
 501   return num_code_points; 
 502 } 
 503  
 504 inline size_t count_code_points(basic_string_view<char8_type> s) { 
 505   return count_code_points(basic_string_view<char>( 
 506       reinterpret_cast<const char*>(s.data()), s.size())); 
 507 } 
 508  
 509 template <typename Char> 
 510 inline size_t code_point_index(basic_string_view<Char> s, size_t n) { 
 511   size_t size = s.size(); 
 512   return n < size ? n : size; 
 513 } 
 514  
 515 // Calculates the index of the nth code point in a UTF-8 string. 
 516 inline size_t code_point_index(basic_string_view<char8_type> s, size_t n) { 
 517   const char8_type* data = s.data(); 
 518   size_t num_code_points = 0; 
 519   for (size_t i = 0, size = s.size(); i != size; ++i) { 
 520     if ((data[i] & 0xc0) != 0x80 && ++num_code_points > n) { 
 521       return i; 
 522     } 
 523   } 
 524   return s.size(); 
 525 }

 527 template <typename InputIt, typename OutChar> 
 528 using needs_conversion = bool_constant< 
 529     std::is_same<typename std::iterator_traits<InputIt>::value_type, 
 530                  char>::value && 
 531     std::is_same<OutChar, char8_type>::value>; 
 532  
 533 template <typename OutChar, typename InputIt, typename OutputIt, 
 534           FMT_ENABLE_IF(!needs_conversion<InputIt, OutChar>::value)> 
 535 OutputIt copy_str(InputIt begin, InputIt end, OutputIt it) { 
 536   return std::copy(begin, end, it); 
 537 } 
 538  
 539 template <typename OutChar, typename InputIt, typename OutputIt, 
 540           FMT_ENABLE_IF(needs_conversion<InputIt, OutChar>::value)> 
 541 OutputIt copy_str(InputIt begin, InputIt end, OutputIt it) { 
 542   return std::transform(begin, end, it, 
 543                         [](char c) { return static_cast<char8_type>(c); }); 
 544 }

 546 #ifndef FMT_USE_GRISU 
 547 #  define FMT_USE_GRISU 1 
 548 #endif 
 549  
 550 template <typename T> constexpr bool use_grisu() { 
 551   return FMT_USE_GRISU && std::numeric_limits<double>::is_iec559 && 
 552          sizeof(T) <= sizeof(double); 
 553 } 
 554  
 555 template <typename T> 
 556 template <typename U> 
 557 void buffer<T>::append(const U* begin, const U* end) { 
 558   do { 
 559     auto count = to_unsigned(end - begin); 
 560     try_reserve(size_ + count); 
 561     auto free_cap = capacity_ - size_; 
 562     if (free_cap < count) count = free_cap; 
 563     std::uninitialized_copy_n(begin, count, make_checked(ptr_ + size_, count)); 
 564     size_ += count; 
 565     begin += count; 
 566   } while (begin != end); 
 567 } 
 568  
 569 template <typename OutputIt, typename T, typename Traits> 
 570 void iterator_buffer<OutputIt, T, Traits>::flush() { 
 571   out_ = std::copy_n(data_, this->limit(this->size()), out_); 
 572   this->clear(); 
 573 } 
 574 }  // namespace detail 
 575  
 576 // The number of characters to store in the basic_memory_buffer object itself 
 577 // to avoid dynamic memory allocation. 
 578 enum { inline_buffer_size = 500 }; 
 579  
 580 /** 
 581   \rst 
 582   A dynamically growing memory buffer for trivially copyable/constructible types 
 583   with the first ``SIZE`` elements stored in the object itself. 
 584  
 585   You can use one of the following type aliases for common character types: 
 586  
 587   +----------------+------------------------------+ 
 588   | Type           | Definition                   | 
 589   +================+==============================+ 
 590   | memory_buffer  | basic_memory_buffer<char>    | 
 591   +----------------+------------------------------+ 
 592   | wmemory_buffer | basic_memory_buffer<wchar_t> | 
 593   +----------------+------------------------------+ 
 594  
 595   **Example**:: 
 596  
 597      fmt::memory_buffer out; 
 598      format_to(out, "The answer is {}.", 42); 
 599  
 600   This will append the following output to the ``out`` object: 
 601  
 602   .. code-block:: none 
 603  
 604      The answer is 42. 
 605  
 606   The output can be converted to an ``std::string`` with ``to_string(out)``. 
 607   \endrst 
 608  */ 
 609 template <typename T, size_t SIZE = inline_buffer_size, 
 610           typename Allocator = std::allocator<T>> 
 611 class basic_memory_buffer : public detail::buffer<T> { 
 612  private: 
 613   T store_[SIZE]; 
 614  
 615   // Don't inherit from Allocator avoid generating type_info for it. 
 616   Allocator alloc_; 
 617  
 618   // Deallocate memory allocated by the buffer. 
 619   void deallocate() { 
 620     T* data = this->data(); 
 621     if (data != store_) alloc_.deallocate(data, this->capacity()); 
 622   } 
 623  
 624  protected: 
 625   void grow(size_t size) final; 
 626  
 627  public: 
 628   using value_type = T; 
 629   using const_reference = const T&; 
 630  
 631   explicit basic_memory_buffer(const Allocator& alloc = Allocator()) 
 632       : alloc_(alloc) { 
 633     this->set(store_, SIZE); 
 634   } 
 635   ~basic_memory_buffer() { deallocate(); } 
 636  
 637  private: 
 638   // Move data from other to this buffer. 
 639   void move(basic_memory_buffer& other) { 
 640     alloc_ = std::move(other.alloc_); 
 641     T* data = other.data(); 
 642     size_t size = other.size(), capacity = other.capacity(); 
 643     if (data == other.store_) { 
 644       this->set(store_, capacity); 
 645       std::uninitialized_copy(other.store_, other.store_ + size, 
 646                               detail::make_checked(store_, capacity)); 
 647     } else { 
 648       this->set(data, capacity); 
 649       // Set pointer to the inline array so that delete is not called 
 650       // when deallocating. 
 651       other.set(other.store_, 0); 
 652     } 
 653     this->resize(size); 
 654   } 
 655  
 656  public: 
 657   /** 
 658     \rst 
 659     Constructs a :class:`fmt::basic_memory_buffer` object moving the content 
 660     of the other object to it. 
 661     \endrst 
 662    */ 
 663   basic_memory_buffer(basic_memory_buffer&& other) FMT_NOEXCEPT { move(other); } 
 664  
 665   /** 
 666     \rst 
 667     Moves the content of the other ``basic_memory_buffer`` object to this one. 
 668     \endrst 
 669    */ 
 670   basic_memory_buffer& operator=(basic_memory_buffer&& other) FMT_NOEXCEPT { 
 671     FMT_ASSERT(this != &other, ""); 
 672     deallocate(); 
 673     move(other); 
 674     return *this; 
 675   } 
 676  
 677   // Returns a copy of the allocator associated with this buffer. 
 678   Allocator get_allocator() const { return alloc_; } 
 679  
 680   /** 
 681     Resizes the buffer to contain *count* elements. If T is a POD type new 
 682     elements may not be initialized. 
 683    */ 
 684   void resize(size_t count) { this->try_resize(count); } 
 685  
 686   /** Increases the buffer capacity to *new_capacity*. */ 
 687   void reserve(size_t new_capacity) { this->try_reserve(new_capacity); } 
 688 }; 
 689  
 690 template <typename T, size_t SIZE, typename Allocator> 
 691 void basic_memory_buffer<T, SIZE, Allocator>::grow(size_t size) { 
 692 #ifdef FMT_FUZZ 
 693   if (size > 5000) throw std::runtime_error("fuzz mode - won't grow that much"); 
 694 #endif 
 695   size_t old_capacity = this->capacity(); 
 696   size_t new_capacity = old_capacity + old_capacity / 2; 
 697   if (size > new_capacity) new_capacity = size; 
 698   T* old_data = this->data(); 
 699   T* new_data = 
 700       std::allocator_traits<Allocator>::allocate(alloc_, new_capacity); 
 701   // The following code doesn't throw, so the raw pointer above doesn't leak. 
 702   std::uninitialized_copy(old_data, old_data + this->size(), 
 703                           detail::make_checked(new_data, new_capacity)); 
 704   this->set(new_data, new_capacity); 
 705   // deallocate must not throw according to the standard, but even if it does, 
 706   // the buffer already uses the new storage and will deallocate it in 
 707   // destructor. 
 708   if (old_data != store_) alloc_.deallocate(old_data, old_capacity); 
 709 } 
 710  
 711 using memory_buffer = basic_memory_buffer<char>; 
 712 using wmemory_buffer = basic_memory_buffer<wchar_t>; 
 713  
 714 template <typename T, size_t SIZE, typename Allocator> 
 715 struct is_contiguous<basic_memory_buffer<T, SIZE, Allocator>> : std::true_type { 
 716 };

 734 template <typename T> 
 735 using is_signed = 
 736     std::integral_constant<bool, std::numeric_limits<T>::is_signed || 
 737                                      std::is_same<T, int128_t>::value>;

 739 // Returns true if value is negative, false otherwise. 
 740 // Same as `value < 0` but doesn't produce warnings if T is an unsigned type. 
 741 template <typename T, FMT_ENABLE_IF(is_signed<T>::value)> 
 742 FMT_CONSTEXPR bool is_negative(T value) { 
 743   return value < 0; 
 744 } 
 745 template <typename T, FMT_ENABLE_IF(!is_signed<T>::value)> 
 746 FMT_CONSTEXPR bool is_negative(T) { 
 747   return false; 
 748 }

 750 template <typename T, FMT_ENABLE_IF(std::is_floating_point<T>::value)> 
 751 FMT_CONSTEXPR bool is_supported_floating_point(T) { 
 752   return (std::is_same<T, float>::value && FMT_USE_FLOAT) || 
 753          (std::is_same<T, double>::value && FMT_USE_DOUBLE) || 
 754          (std::is_same<T, long double>::value && FMT_USE_LONG_DOUBLE); 
 755 }

 757 #if FMT_REDUCE_INT_INSTANTIATIONS 
 758 // Pick the largest integer container to represent all values of T. 
 759 template <typename T> 
 760 using uint32_or_64_or_128_t = 
 761     conditional_t<sizeof(uint128_t) < sizeof(uint64_t), uint64_t, uint128_t>; 
 762 #else 
 763 // Smallest of uint32_t, uint64_t, uint128_t that is large enough to 
 764 // represent all values of T. 
 765 template <typename T> 
 766 using uint32_or_64_or_128_t = 
 767     conditional_t<num_bits<T>() <= 32, uint32_t, 
 768                   conditional_t<num_bits<T>() <= 64, uint64_t, uint128_t>>; 
 769 #endif

 771 // Static data is placed in this class template for the header-only config. 
 772 template <typename T = void> struct FMT_EXTERN_TEMPLATE_API basic_data { 
 773   // Maps the result of bsr(n) to ceil(log10(n)). 
 774   static const uint16_t bsr2log10[]; 
 775   static const uint64_t powers_of_10_64[]; 
 776   static const uint32_t zero_or_powers_of_10_32[]; 
 777   static const uint64_t zero_or_powers_of_10_64[]; 
 778   static const uint64_t pow10_significands[]; 
 779   static const int16_t pow10_exponents[]; 
 780   // GCC generates slightly better code for pairs than chars. 
 781   using digit_pair = char[2]; 
 782   static const digit_pair digits[]; 
 783   static const char hex_digits[]; 
 784   static const char foreground_color[]; 
 785   static const char background_color[]; 
 786   static const char reset_color[5]; 
 787   static const wchar_t wreset_color[5]; 
 788   static const char signs[]; 
 789   static const char left_padding_shifts[5]; 
 790   static const char right_padding_shifts[5]; 
 791 };

 797 // This is a struct rather than an alias to avoid shadowing warnings in gcc.

 800 #ifdef FMT_BUILTIN_CLZLL 
 801 // Returns the number of decimal digits in n. Leading zeros are not counted 
 802 // except for n == 0 in which case count_digits returns 1. 
 803 inline int count_digits(uint64_t n) { 
 804   // https://github.com/fmtlib/format-benchmark/blob/master/digits10 
 805   auto t = data::bsr2log10[FMT_BUILTIN_CLZLL(n | 1) ^ 63]; 
 806   return t - (n < data::zero_or_powers_of_10_64[t - 1]); 
 807 } 
 808 #else 
 809 // Fallback version of count_digits used when __builtin_clz is not available. 
 810 inline int count_digits(uint64_t n) { 
 811   int count = 1; 
 812   for (;;) { 
 813     // Integer division is slow so do it for a group of four digits instead 
 814     // of for every digit. The idea comes from the talk by Alexandrescu 
 815     // "Three Optimization Tips for C++". See speed-test for a comparison. 
 816     if (n < 10) return count; 
 817     if (n < 100) return count + 1; 
 818     if (n < 1000) return count + 2; 
 819     if (n < 10000) return count + 3; 
 820     n /= 10000u; 
 821     count += 4; 
 822   } 
 823 } 
 824 #endif 
 825  
 826 #if FMT_USE_INT128 
 827 inline int count_digits(uint128_t n) { 
 828   int count = 1; 
 829   for (;;) { 
 830     // Integer division is slow so do it for a group of four digits instead 
 831     // of for every digit. The idea comes from the talk by Alexandrescu 
 832     // "Three Optimization Tips for C++". See speed-test for a comparison. 
 833     if (n < 10) return count; 
 834     if (n < 100) return count + 1; 
 835     if (n < 1000) return count + 2; 
 836     if (n < 10000) return count + 3; 
 837     n /= 10000U; 
 838     count += 4; 
 839   } 
 840 } 
 841 #endif 
 842  
 843 // Counts the number of digits in n. BITS = log2(radix). 
 844 template <unsigned BITS, typename UInt> inline int count_digits(UInt n) { 
 845   int num_digits = 0; 
 846   do { 
 847     ++num_digits; 
 848   } while ((n >>= BITS) != 0); 
 849   return num_digits; 
 850 } 
 851  
 852 template <> int count_digits<4>(detail::fallback_uintptr n); 
 853  
 854 #if FMT_GCC_VERSION || FMT_CLANG_VERSION 
 855 #  define FMT_ALWAYS_INLINE inline __attribute__((always_inline)) 
 856 #else 
 857 #  define FMT_ALWAYS_INLINE 
 858 #endif 
 859  
 860 #ifdef FMT_BUILTIN_CLZ 
 861 // Optional version of count_digits for better performance on 32-bit platforms. 
 862 inline int count_digits(uint32_t n) { 
 863   auto t = data::bsr2log10[FMT_BUILTIN_CLZ(n | 1) ^ 31]; 
 864   return t - (n < data::zero_or_powers_of_10_32[t - 1]); 
 865 } 
 866 #endif

 869   return std::numeric_limits<Int>::digits10; 
 870 } 
 871 template <> constexpr int digits10<int128_t>() FMT_NOEXCEPT { return 38; } 
 872 template <> constexpr int digits10<uint128_t>() FMT_NOEXCEPT { return 38; }

 898 // Compares two characters for equality. 
 899 template <typename Char> bool equal2(const Char* lhs, const char* rhs) { 
 900   return lhs[0] == rhs[0] && lhs[1] == rhs[1]; 
 901 } 
 902 inline bool equal2(const char* lhs, const char* rhs) { 
 903   return memcmp(lhs, rhs, 2) == 0; 
 904 } 
 905  
 906 // Copies two characters from src to dst. 
 907 template <typename Char> void copy2(Char* dst, const char* src) { 
 908   *dst++ = static_cast<Char>(*src++); 
 909   *dst = static_cast<Char>(*src); 
 910 } 
 911 inline void copy2(char* dst, const char* src) { memcpy(dst, src, 2); } 
 912  
 913 template <typename Iterator> struct format_decimal_result { 
 914   Iterator begin; 
 915   Iterator end; 
 916 }; 
 917  
 918 // Formats a decimal unsigned integer value writing into out pointing to a 
 919 // buffer of specified size. The caller must ensure that the buffer is large 
 920 // enough. 
 921 template <typename Char, typename UInt> 
 922 inline format_decimal_result<Char*> format_decimal(Char* out, UInt value, 
 923                                                    int size) { 
 924   FMT_ASSERT(size >= count_digits(value), "invalid digit count"); 
 925   out += size; 
 926   Char* end = out; 
 927   while (value >= 100) { 
 928     // Integer division is slow so do it for a group of two digits instead 
 929     // of for every digit. The idea comes from the talk by Alexandrescu 
 930     // "Three Optimization Tips for C++". See speed-test for a comparison. 
 931     out -= 2; 
 932     copy2(out, data::digits[value % 100]); 
 933     value /= 100; 
 934   } 
 935   if (value < 10) { 
 936     *--out = static_cast<Char>('0' + value); 
 937     return {out, end}; 
 938   } 
 939   out -= 2; 
 940   copy2(out, data::digits[value]); 
 941   return {out, end}; 
 942 } 
 943  
 944 template <typename Char, typename UInt, typename Iterator, 
 945           FMT_ENABLE_IF(!std::is_pointer<remove_cvref_t<Iterator>>::value)> 
 946 inline format_decimal_result<Iterator> format_decimal(Iterator out, UInt value, 
 947                                                       int num_digits) { 
 948   // Buffer should be large enough to hold all digits (<= digits10 + 1). 
 949   enum { max_size = digits10<UInt>() + 1 }; 
 950   Char buffer[2 * max_size]; 
 951   auto end = format_decimal(buffer, value, num_digits).end; 
 952   return {out, detail::copy_str<Char>(buffer, end, out)}; 
 953 } 
 954  
 955 template <unsigned BASE_BITS, typename Char, typename UInt> 
 956 inline Char* format_uint(Char* buffer, UInt value, int num_digits, 
 957                          bool upper = false) { 
 958   buffer += num_digits; 
 959   Char* end = buffer; 
 960   do { 
 961     const char* digits = upper ? "0123456789ABCDEF" : data::hex_digits; 
 962     unsigned digit = (value & ((1 << BASE_BITS) - 1)); 
 963     *--buffer = static_cast<Char>(BASE_BITS < 4 ? static_cast<char>('0' + digit) 
 964                                                 : digits[digit]); 
 965   } while ((value >>= BASE_BITS) != 0); 
 966   return end; 
 967 } 
 968  
 969 template <unsigned BASE_BITS, typename Char> 
 970 Char* format_uint(Char* buffer, detail::fallback_uintptr n, int num_digits, 
 971                   bool = false) { 
 972   auto char_digits = std::numeric_limits<unsigned char>::digits / 4; 
 973   int start = (num_digits + char_digits - 1) / char_digits - 1; 
 974   if (int start_digits = num_digits % char_digits) { 
 975     unsigned value = n.value[start--]; 
 976     buffer = format_uint<BASE_BITS>(buffer, value, start_digits); 
 977   } 
 978   for (; start >= 0; --start) { 
 979     unsigned value = n.value[start]; 
 980     buffer += char_digits; 
 981     auto p = buffer; 
 982     for (int i = 0; i < char_digits; ++i) { 
 983       unsigned digit = (value & ((1 << BASE_BITS) - 1)); 
 984       *--p = static_cast<Char>(data::hex_digits[digit]); 
 985       value >>= BASE_BITS; 
 986     } 
 987   } 
 988   return buffer; 
 989 } 
 990  
 991 template <unsigned BASE_BITS, typename Char, typename It, typename UInt> 
 992 inline It format_uint(It out, UInt value, int num_digits, bool upper = false) { 
 993   // Buffer should be large enough to hold all digits (digits / BASE_BITS + 1). 
 994   char buffer[num_bits<UInt>() / BASE_BITS + 1]; 
 995   format_uint<BASE_BITS>(buffer, value, num_digits, upper); 
 996   return detail::copy_str<Char>(buffer, buffer + num_digits, out); 
 997 }

1001  private:

1003

1006   operator wstring_view() const { return {&buffer_[0], size()}; } 
1007   size_t size() const { return buffer_.size() - 1; } 
1008   const wchar_t* c_str() const { return &buffer_[0]; } 
1009   std::wstring str() const { return {&buffer_[0], size()}; }

1014 // Workaround an array initialization issue in gcc 4.8. 
1015 template <typename Char> struct fill_t { 
1016  private: 
1017   enum { max_size = 4 }; 
1018   Char data_[max_size]; 
1019   unsigned char size_; 
1020  
1021  public: 
1022   FMT_CONSTEXPR void operator=(basic_string_view<Char> s) { 
1023     auto size = s.size(); 
1024     if (size > max_size) { 
1025       FMT_THROW(format_error("invalid fill")); 
1026       return; 
1027     } 
1028     for (size_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) data_[i] = s[i]; 
1029     size_ = static_cast<unsigned char>(size); 
1030   } 
1031  
1032   size_t size() const { return size_; } 
1033   const Char* data() const { return data_; } 
1034  
1035   FMT_CONSTEXPR Char& operator[](size_t index) { return data_[index]; } 
1036   FMT_CONSTEXPR const Char& operator[](size_t index) const { 
1037     return data_[index]; 
1038   } 
1039  
1040   static FMT_CONSTEXPR fill_t<Char> make() { 
1041     auto fill = fill_t<Char>(); 
1042     fill[0] = Char(' '); 
1043     fill.size_ = 1; 
1044     return fill; 
1045   } 
1046 }; 
1047 }  // namespace detail

1061 // Format specifiers for built-in and string types. 
1062 template <typename Char> struct basic_format_specs { 
1063   int width; 
1064   int precision; 
1065   char type; 
1066   align_t align : 4; 
1067   sign_t sign : 3; 
1068   bool alt : 1;  // Alternate form ('#'). 
1069   detail::fill_t<Char> fill; 
1070  
1071   constexpr basic_format_specs() 
1072       : width(0), 
1073         precision(-1), 
1074         type(0), 
1075         align(align::none), 
1076         sign(sign::none), 
1077         alt(false), 
1078         fill(detail::fill_t<Char>::make()) {} 
1079 };

1081 using format_specs = basic_format_specs<char>;

1083 namespace detail { 
1084  
1085 // A floating-point presentation format. 
1086 enum class float_format : unsigned char { 
1087   general,  // General: exponent notation or fixed point based on magnitude. 
1088   exp,      // Exponent notation with the default precision of 6, e.g. 1.2e-3. 
1089   fixed,    // Fixed point with the default precision of 6, e.g. 0.0012. 
1090   hex 
1091 }; 
1092  
1093 struct float_specs { 
1094   int precision; 
1095   float_format format : 8; 
1096   sign_t sign : 8; 
1097   bool upper : 1; 
1098   bool locale : 1; 
1099   bool binary32 : 1; 
1100   bool use_grisu : 1; 
1101   bool showpoint : 1; 
1102 }; 
1103  
1104 // Writes the exponent exp in the form "[+-]d{2,3}" to buffer. 
1105 template <typename Char, typename It> It write_exponent(int exp, It it) { 
1106   FMT_ASSERT(-10000 < exp && exp < 10000, "exponent out of range"); 
1107   if (exp < 0) { 
1108     *it++ = static_cast<Char>('-'); 
1109     exp = -exp; 
1110   } else { 
1111     *it++ = static_cast<Char>('+'); 
1112   } 
1113   if (exp >= 100) { 
1114     const char* top = data::digits[exp / 100]; 
1115     if (exp >= 1000) *it++ = static_cast<Char>(top[0]); 
1116     *it++ = static_cast<Char>(top[1]); 
1117     exp %= 100; 
1118   } 
1119   const char* d = data::digits[exp]; 
1120   *it++ = static_cast<Char>(d[0]); 
1121   *it++ = static_cast<Char>(d[1]); 
1122   return it; 
1123 } 
1124  
1125 template <typename Char> class float_writer { 
1126  private: 
1127   // The number is given as v = digits_ * pow(10, exp_). 
1128   const char* digits_; 
1129   int num_digits_; 
1130   int exp_; 
1131   size_t size_; 
1132   float_specs specs_; 
1133   Char decimal_point_; 
1134  
1135   template <typename It> It prettify(It it) const { 
1136     // pow(10, full_exp - 1) <= v <= pow(10, full_exp). 
1137     int full_exp = num_digits_ + exp_; 
1138     if (specs_.format == float_format::exp) { 
1139       // Insert a decimal point after the first digit and add an exponent. 
1140       *it++ = static_cast<Char>(*digits_); 
1141       int num_zeros = specs_.precision - num_digits_; 
1142       if (num_digits_ > 1 || specs_.showpoint) *it++ = decimal_point_; 
1143       it = copy_str<Char>(digits_ + 1, digits_ + num_digits_, it); 
1144       if (num_zeros > 0 && specs_.showpoint) 
1145         it = std::fill_n(it, num_zeros, static_cast<Char>('0')); 
1146       *it++ = static_cast<Char>(specs_.upper ? 'E' : 'e'); 
1147       return write_exponent<Char>(full_exp - 1, it); 
1148     } 
1149     if (num_digits_ <= full_exp) { 
1150       // 1234e7 -> 12340000000[.0+] 
1151       it = copy_str<Char>(digits_, digits_ + num_digits_, it); 
1152       it = std::fill_n(it, full_exp - num_digits_, static_cast<Char>('0')); 
1153       if (specs_.showpoint || specs_.precision < 0) { 
1154         *it++ = decimal_point_; 
1155         int num_zeros = specs_.precision - full_exp; 
1156         if (num_zeros <= 0) { 
1157           if (specs_.format != float_format::fixed) 
1158             *it++ = static_cast<Char>('0'); 
1159           return it; 
1160         } 
1161 #ifdef FMT_FUZZ 
1162         if (num_zeros > 5000) 
1163           throw std::runtime_error("fuzz mode - avoiding excessive cpu use"); 
1164 #endif 
1165         it = std::fill_n(it, num_zeros, static_cast<Char>('0')); 
1166       } 
1167     } else if (full_exp > 0) { 
1168       // 1234e-2 -> 12.34[0+] 
1169       it = copy_str<Char>(digits_, digits_ + full_exp, it); 
1170       if (!specs_.showpoint) { 
1171         // Remove trailing zeros. 
1172         int num_digits = num_digits_; 
1173         while (num_digits > full_exp && digits_[num_digits - 1] == '0') 
1174           --num_digits; 
1175         if (num_digits != full_exp) *it++ = decimal_point_; 
1176         return copy_str<Char>(digits_ + full_exp, digits_ + num_digits, it); 
1177       } 
1178       *it++ = decimal_point_; 
1179       it = copy_str<Char>(digits_ + full_exp, digits_ + num_digits_, it); 
1180       if (specs_.precision > num_digits_) { 
1181         // Add trailing zeros. 
1182         int num_zeros = specs_.precision - num_digits_; 
1183         it = std::fill_n(it, num_zeros, static_cast<Char>('0')); 
1184       } 
1185     } else { 
1186       // 1234e-6 -> 0.001234 
1187       *it++ = static_cast<Char>('0'); 
1188       int num_zeros = -full_exp; 
1189       int num_digits = num_digits_; 
1190       if (num_digits == 0 && specs_.precision >= 0 && 
1191           specs_.precision < num_zeros) { 
1192         num_zeros = specs_.precision; 
1193       } 
1194       // Remove trailing zeros. 
1195       if (!specs_.showpoint) 
1196         while (num_digits > 0 && digits_[num_digits - 1] == '0') --num_digits; 
1197       if (num_zeros != 0 || num_digits != 0 || specs_.showpoint) { 
1198         *it++ = decimal_point_; 
1199         it = std::fill_n(it, num_zeros, static_cast<Char>('0')); 
1200         it = copy_str<Char>(digits_, digits_ + num_digits, it); 
1201       } 
1202     } 
1203     return it; 
1204   } 
1205  
1206  public: 
1207   float_writer(const char* digits, int num_digits, int exp, float_specs specs, 
1208                Char decimal_point) 
1209       : digits_(digits), 
1210         num_digits_(num_digits), 
1211         exp_(exp), 
1212         specs_(specs), 
1213         decimal_point_(decimal_point) { 
1214     int full_exp = num_digits + exp - 1; 
1215     int precision = specs.precision > 0 ? specs.precision : 16; 
1216     if (specs_.format == float_format::general && 
1217         !(full_exp >= -4 && full_exp < precision)) { 
1218       specs_.format = float_format::exp; 
1219     } 
1220     size_ = prettify(counting_iterator()).count(); 
1221     size_ += specs.sign ? 1 : 0; 
1222   } 
1223  
1224   size_t size() const { return size_; } 
1225  
1226   template <typename It> It operator()(It it) const { 
1227     if (specs_.sign) *it++ = static_cast<Char>(data::signs[specs_.sign]); 
1228     return prettify(it); 
1229   } 
1230 }; 
1231  
1232 template <typename T> 
1233 int format_float(T value, int precision, float_specs specs, buffer<char>& buf); 
1234  
1235 // Formats a floating-point number with snprintf. 
1236 template <typename T> 
1237 int snprintf_float(T value, int precision, float_specs specs, 
1238                    buffer<char>& buf); 
1239  
1240 template <typename T> T promote_float(T value) { return value; } 
1241 inline double promote_float(float value) { return static_cast<double>(value); } 
1242  
1243 template <typename Handler> 
1244 FMT_CONSTEXPR void handle_int_type_spec(char spec, Handler&& handler) { 
1245   switch (spec) { 
1246   case 0: 
1247   case 'd': 
1248     handler.on_dec(); 
1249     break; 
1250   case 'x': 
1251   case 'X': 
1252     handler.on_hex(); 
1253     break; 
1254   case 'b': 
1255   case 'B': 
1256     handler.on_bin(); 
1257     break; 
1258   case 'o': 
1259     handler.on_oct(); 
1260     break; 
1261 #ifdef FMT_DEPRECATED_N_SPECIFIER 
1262   case 'n': 
1263 #endif 
1264   case 'L': 
1265     handler.on_num(); 
1266     break; 
1267   case 'c': 
1268     handler.on_chr(); 
1269     break; 
1270   default: 
1271     handler.on_error(); 
1272   } 
1273 } 
1274  
1275 template <typename ErrorHandler = error_handler, typename Char> 
1276 FMT_CONSTEXPR float_specs parse_float_type_spec( 
1277     const basic_format_specs<Char>& specs, ErrorHandler&& eh = {}) { 
1278   auto result = float_specs(); 
1279   result.showpoint = specs.alt; 
1280   switch (specs.type) { 
1281   case 0: 
1282     result.format = float_format::general; 
1283     result.showpoint |= specs.precision > 0; 
1284     break; 
1285   case 'G': 
1286     result.upper = true; 
1287     FMT_FALLTHROUGH; 
1288   case 'g': 
1289     result.format = float_format::general; 
1290     break; 
1291   case 'E': 
1292     result.upper = true; 
1293     FMT_FALLTHROUGH; 
1294   case 'e': 
1295     result.format = float_format::exp; 
1296     result.showpoint |= specs.precision != 0; 
1297     break; 
1298   case 'F': 
1299     result.upper = true; 
1300     FMT_FALLTHROUGH; 
1301   case 'f': 
1302     result.format = float_format::fixed; 
1303     result.showpoint |= specs.precision != 0; 
1304     break; 
1305   case 'A': 
1306     result.upper = true; 
1307     FMT_FALLTHROUGH; 
1308   case 'a': 
1309     result.format = float_format::hex; 
1310     break; 
1311 #ifdef FMT_DEPRECATED_N_SPECIFIER 
1312   case 'n': 
1313 #endif 
1314   case 'L': 
1315     result.locale = true; 
1316     break; 
1317   default: 
1318     eh.on_error("invalid type specifier"); 
1319     break; 
1320   } 
1321   return result; 
1322 } 
1323  
1324 template <typename Char, typename Handler> 
1325 FMT_CONSTEXPR void handle_char_specs(const basic_format_specs<Char>* specs, 
1326                                      Handler&& handler) { 
1327   if (!specs) return handler.on_char(); 
1328   if (specs->type && specs->type != 'c') return handler.on_int(); 
1329   if (specs->align == align::numeric || specs->sign != sign::none || specs->alt) 
1330     handler.on_error("invalid format specifier for char"); 
1331   handler.on_char(); 
1332 } 
1333  
1334 template <typename Char, typename Handler> 
1335 FMT_CONSTEXPR void handle_cstring_type_spec(Char spec, Handler&& handler) { 
1336   if (spec == 0 || spec == 's') 
1337     handler.on_string(); 
1338   else if (spec == 'p') 
1339     handler.on_pointer(); 
1340   else 
1341     handler.on_error("invalid type specifier"); 
1342 } 
1343  
1344 template <typename Char, typename ErrorHandler> 
1345 FMT_CONSTEXPR void check_string_type_spec(Char spec, ErrorHandler&& eh) { 
1346   if (spec != 0 && spec != 's') eh.on_error("invalid type specifier"); 
1347 } 
1348  
1349 template <typename Char, typename ErrorHandler> 
1350 FMT_CONSTEXPR void check_pointer_type_spec(Char spec, ErrorHandler&& eh) { 
1351   if (spec != 0 && spec != 'p') eh.on_error("invalid type specifier"); 
1352 } 
1353  
1354 template <typename ErrorHandler> class int_type_checker : private ErrorHandler { 
1355  public: 
1356   FMT_CONSTEXPR explicit int_type_checker(ErrorHandler eh) : ErrorHandler(eh) {} 
1357  
1358   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_dec() {} 
1359   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_hex() {} 
1360   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_bin() {} 
1361   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_oct() {} 
1362   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_num() {} 
1363   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_chr() {} 
1364  
1365   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_error() { 
1366     ErrorHandler::on_error("invalid type specifier"); 
1367   } 
1368 }; 
1369  
1370 template <typename ErrorHandler> 
1371 class char_specs_checker : public ErrorHandler { 
1372  private: 
1373   char type_; 
1374  
1375  public: 
1376   FMT_CONSTEXPR char_specs_checker(char type, ErrorHandler eh) 
1377       : ErrorHandler(eh), type_(type) {} 
1378  
1379   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_int() { 
1380     handle_int_type_spec(type_, int_type_checker<ErrorHandler>(*this)); 
1381   } 
1382   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_char() {} 
1383 };

1385 template <typename ErrorHandler> 
1386 class cstring_type_checker : public ErrorHandler { 
1387  public: 
1388   FMT_CONSTEXPR explicit cstring_type_checker(ErrorHandler eh) 
1389       : ErrorHandler(eh) {} 
1390  
1391   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_string() {} 
1392   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_pointer() {} 
1393 }; 
1394  
1395 template <typename OutputIt, typename Char> 
1396 FMT_NOINLINE OutputIt fill(OutputIt it, size_t n, const fill_t<Char>& fill) { 
1397   auto fill_size = fill.size(); 
1398   if (fill_size == 1) return std::fill_n(it, n, fill[0]); 
1399   for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) it = std::copy_n(fill.data(), fill_size, it); 
1400   return it; 
1401 } 
1402  
1403 // Writes the output of f, padded according to format specifications in specs. 
1404 // size: output size in code units. 
1405 // width: output display width in (terminal) column positions. 
1406 template <align::type align = align::left, typename OutputIt, typename Char, 
1407           typename F> 
1408 inline OutputIt write_padded(OutputIt out, 
1409                              const basic_format_specs<Char>& specs, size_t size, 
1410                              size_t width, const F& f) { 
1411   static_assert(align == align::left || align == align::right, ""); 
1412   unsigned spec_width = to_unsigned(specs.width); 
1413   size_t padding = spec_width > width ? spec_width - width : 0; 
1414   auto* shifts = align == align::left ? data::left_padding_shifts 
1415                                       : data::right_padding_shifts; 
1416   size_t left_padding = padding >> shifts[specs.align]; 
1417   auto it = reserve(out, size + padding * specs.fill.size()); 
1418   it = fill(it, left_padding, specs.fill); 
1419   it = f(it); 
1420   it = fill(it, padding - left_padding, specs.fill); 
1421   return base_iterator(out, it); 
1422 } 
1423  
1424 template <align::type align = align::left, typename OutputIt, typename Char, 
1425           typename F> 
1426 inline OutputIt write_padded(OutputIt out, 
1427                              const basic_format_specs<Char>& specs, size_t size, 
1428                              const F& f) { 
1429   return write_padded<align>(out, specs, size, size, f); 
1430 } 
1431  
1432 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt> 
1433 OutputIt write_bytes(OutputIt out, string_view bytes, 
1434                      const basic_format_specs<Char>& specs) { 
1435   using iterator = remove_reference_t<decltype(reserve(out, 0))>; 
1436   return write_padded(out, specs, bytes.size(), [bytes](iterator it) { 
1437     const char* data = bytes.data(); 
1438     return copy_str<Char>(data, data + bytes.size(), it); 
1439   }); 
1440 } 
1441  
1442 // Data for write_int that doesn't depend on output iterator type. It is used to 
1443 // avoid template code bloat. 
1444 template <typename Char> struct write_int_data { 
1445   size_t size; 
1446   size_t padding; 
1447  
1448   write_int_data(int num_digits, string_view prefix, 
1449                  const basic_format_specs<Char>& specs) 
1450       : size(prefix.size() + to_unsigned(num_digits)), padding(0) { 
1451     if (specs.align == align::numeric) { 
1452       auto width = to_unsigned(specs.width); 
1453       if (width > size) { 
1454         padding = width - size; 
1455         size = width; 
1456       } 
1457     } else if (specs.precision > num_digits) { 
1458       size = prefix.size() + to_unsigned(specs.precision); 
1459       padding = to_unsigned(specs.precision - num_digits); 
1460     } 
1461   } 
1462 }; 
1463  
1464 // Writes an integer in the format 
1465 //   <left-padding><prefix><numeric-padding><digits><right-padding> 
1466 // where <digits> are written by f(it). 
1467 template <typename OutputIt, typename Char, typename F> 
1468 OutputIt write_int(OutputIt out, int num_digits, string_view prefix, 
1469                    const basic_format_specs<Char>& specs, F f) { 
1470   auto data = write_int_data<Char>(num_digits, prefix, specs); 
1471   using iterator = remove_reference_t<decltype(reserve(out, 0))>; 
1472   return write_padded<align::right>(out, specs, data.size, [=](iterator it) { 
1473     if (prefix.size() != 0) 
1474       it = copy_str<Char>(prefix.begin(), prefix.end(), it); 
1475     it = std::fill_n(it, data.padding, static_cast<Char>('0')); 
1476     return f(it); 
1477   }); 
1478 } 
1479  
1480 template <typename StrChar, typename Char, typename OutputIt> 
1481 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, basic_string_view<StrChar> s, 
1482                const basic_format_specs<Char>& specs) { 
1483   auto data = s.data(); 
1484   auto size = s.size(); 
1485   if (specs.precision >= 0 && to_unsigned(specs.precision) < size) 
1486     size = code_point_index(s, to_unsigned(specs.precision)); 
1487   auto width = specs.width != 0 
1488                    ? count_code_points(basic_string_view<StrChar>(data, size)) 
1489                    : 0; 
1490   using iterator = remove_reference_t<decltype(reserve(out, 0))>; 
1491   return write_padded(out, specs, size, width, [=](iterator it) { 
1492     return copy_str<Char>(data, data + size, it); 
1493   }); 
1494 } 
1495  
1496 // The handle_int_type_spec handler that writes an integer. 
1497 template <typename OutputIt, typename Char, typename UInt> struct int_writer { 
1498   OutputIt out; 
1499   locale_ref locale; 
1500   const basic_format_specs<Char>& specs; 
1501   UInt abs_value; 
1502   char prefix[4]; 
1503   unsigned prefix_size; 
1504  
1505   using iterator = 
1506       remove_reference_t<decltype(reserve(std::declval<OutputIt&>(), 0))>; 
1507  
1508   string_view get_prefix() const { return string_view(prefix, prefix_size); } 
1509  
1510   template <typename Int> 
1511   int_writer(OutputIt output, locale_ref loc, Int value, 
1512              const basic_format_specs<Char>& s) 
1513       : out(output), 
1514         locale(loc), 
1515         specs(s), 
1516         abs_value(static_cast<UInt>(value)), 
1517         prefix_size(0) { 
1518     static_assert(std::is_same<uint32_or_64_or_128_t<Int>, UInt>::value, ""); 
1519     if (is_negative(value)) { 
1520       prefix[0] = '-'; 
1521       ++prefix_size; 
1522       abs_value = 0 - abs_value; 
1523     } else if (specs.sign != sign::none && specs.sign != sign::minus) { 
1524       prefix[0] = specs.sign == sign::plus ? '+' : ' '; 
1525       ++prefix_size; 
1526     } 
1527   } 
1528  
1529   void on_dec() { 
1530     auto num_digits = count_digits(abs_value); 
1531     out = write_int( 
1532         out, num_digits, get_prefix(), specs, [this, num_digits](iterator it) { 
1533           return format_decimal<Char>(it, abs_value, num_digits).end; 
1534         }); 
1535   } 
1536  
1537   void on_hex() { 
1538     if (specs.alt) { 
1539       prefix[prefix_size++] = '0'; 
1540       prefix[prefix_size++] = specs.type; 
1541     } 
1542     int num_digits = count_digits<4>(abs_value); 
1543     out = write_int(out, num_digits, get_prefix(), specs, 
1544                     [this, num_digits](iterator it) { 
1545                       return format_uint<4, Char>(it, abs_value, num_digits, 
1546                                                   specs.type != 'x'); 
1547                     }); 
1548   } 
1549  
1550   void on_bin() { 
1551     if (specs.alt) { 
1552       prefix[prefix_size++] = '0'; 
1553       prefix[prefix_size++] = static_cast<char>(specs.type); 
1554     } 
1555     int num_digits = count_digits<1>(abs_value); 
1556     out = write_int(out, num_digits, get_prefix(), specs, 
1557                     [this, num_digits](iterator it) { 
1558                       return format_uint<1, Char>(it, abs_value, num_digits); 
1559                     }); 
1560   } 
1561  
1562   void on_oct() { 
1563     int num_digits = count_digits<3>(abs_value); 
1564     if (specs.alt && specs.precision <= num_digits && abs_value != 0) { 
1565       // Octal prefix '0' is counted as a digit, so only add it if precision 
1566       // is not greater than the number of digits. 
1567       prefix[prefix_size++] = '0'; 
1568     } 
1569     out = write_int(out, num_digits, get_prefix(), specs, 
1570                     [this, num_digits](iterator it) { 
1571                       return format_uint<3, Char>(it, abs_value, num_digits); 
1572                     }); 
1573   } 
1574  
1575   enum { sep_size = 1 }; 
1576  
1577   void on_num() { 
1578     std::string groups = grouping<Char>(locale); 
1579     if (groups.empty()) return on_dec(); 
1580     auto sep = thousands_sep<Char>(locale); 
1581     if (!sep) return on_dec(); 
1582     int num_digits = count_digits(abs_value); 
1583     int size = num_digits, n = num_digits; 
1584     std::string::const_iterator group = groups.cbegin(); 
1585     while (group != groups.cend() && n > *group && *group > 0 && 
1586            *group != max_value<char>()) { 
1587       size += sep_size; 
1588       n -= *group; 
1589       ++group; 
1590     } 
1591     if (group == groups.cend()) size += sep_size * ((n - 1) / groups.back()); 
1592     char digits[40]; 
1593     format_decimal(digits, abs_value, num_digits); 
1594     basic_memory_buffer<Char> buffer; 
1595     size += prefix_size; 
1596     buffer.resize(size); 
1597     basic_string_view<Char> s(&sep, sep_size); 
1598     // Index of a decimal digit with the least significant digit having index 0. 
1599     int digit_index = 0; 
1600     group = groups.cbegin(); 
1601     auto p = buffer.data() + size; 
1602     for (int i = num_digits - 1; i >= 0; --i) { 
1603       *--p = static_cast<Char>(digits[i]); 
1604       if (*group <= 0 || ++digit_index % *group != 0 || 
1605           *group == max_value<char>()) 
1606         continue; 
1607       if (group + 1 != groups.cend()) { 
1608         digit_index = 0; 
1609         ++group; 
1610       } 
1611       p -= s.size(); 
1612       std::uninitialized_copy(s.data(), s.data() + s.size(), 
1613                               make_checked(p, s.size())); 
1614     } 
1615     if (prefix_size != 0) p[-1] = static_cast<Char>('-'); 
1616     out = write(out, basic_string_view<Char>(buffer.data(), buffer.size()), 
1617                 specs); 
1618   } 
1619  
1620   void on_chr() { *out++ = static_cast<Char>(abs_value); } 
1621  
1622   FMT_NORETURN void on_error() { 
1623     FMT_THROW(format_error("invalid type specifier")); 
1624   } 
1625 }; 
1626  
1627 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt> 
1628 OutputIt write_nonfinite(OutputIt out, bool isinf, 
1629                          const basic_format_specs<Char>& specs, 
1630                          const float_specs& fspecs) { 
1631   auto str = 
1632       isinf ? (fspecs.upper ? "INF" : "inf") : (fspecs.upper ? "NAN" : "nan"); 
1633   constexpr size_t str_size = 3; 
1634   auto sign = fspecs.sign; 
1635   auto size = str_size + (sign ? 1 : 0); 
1636   using iterator = remove_reference_t<decltype(reserve(out, 0))>; 
1637   return write_padded(out, specs, size, [=](iterator it) { 
1638     if (sign) *it++ = static_cast<Char>(data::signs[sign]); 
1639     return copy_str<Char>(str, str + str_size, it); 
1640   }); 
1641 } 
1642  
1643 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt, typename T, 
1644           FMT_ENABLE_IF(std::is_floating_point<T>::value)> 
1645 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, T value, basic_format_specs<Char> specs, 
1646                locale_ref loc = {}) { 
1647   if (const_check(!is_supported_floating_point(value))) return out; 
1648   float_specs fspecs = parse_float_type_spec(specs); 
1649   fspecs.sign = specs.sign; 
1650   if (std::signbit(value)) {  // value < 0 is false for NaN so use signbit. 
1651     fspecs.sign = sign::minus; 
1652     value = -value; 
1653   } else if (fspecs.sign == sign::minus) { 
1654     fspecs.sign = sign::none; 
1655   } 
1656  
1657   if (!std::isfinite(value)) 
1658     return write_nonfinite(out, std::isinf(value), specs, fspecs); 
1659  
1660   if (specs.align == align::numeric && fspecs.sign) { 
1661     auto it = reserve(out, 1); 
1662     *it++ = static_cast<Char>(data::signs[fspecs.sign]); 
1663     out = base_iterator(out, it); 
1664     fspecs.sign = sign::none; 
1665     if (specs.width != 0) --specs.width; 
1666   } 
1667  
1668   memory_buffer buffer; 
1669   if (fspecs.format == float_format::hex) { 
1670     if (fspecs.sign) buffer.push_back(data::signs[fspecs.sign]); 
1671     snprintf_float(promote_float(value), specs.precision, fspecs, buffer); 
1672     return write_bytes(out, {buffer.data(), buffer.size()}, specs); 
1673   } 
1674   int precision = specs.precision >= 0 || !specs.type ? specs.precision : 6; 
1675   if (fspecs.format == float_format::exp) { 
1676     if (precision == max_value<int>()) 
1677       FMT_THROW(format_error("number is too big")); 
1678     else 
1679       ++precision; 
1680   } 
1681   if (const_check(std::is_same<T, float>())) fspecs.binary32 = true; 
1682   fspecs.use_grisu = use_grisu<T>(); 
1683   int exp = format_float(promote_float(value), precision, fspecs, buffer); 
1684   fspecs.precision = precision; 
1685   Char point = 
1686       fspecs.locale ? decimal_point<Char>(loc) : static_cast<Char>('.'); 
1687   float_writer<Char> w(buffer.data(), static_cast<int>(buffer.size()), exp, 
1688                        fspecs, point); 
1689   return write_padded<align::right>(out, specs, w.size(), w); 
1690 } 
1691  
1692 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt, typename T, 
1693           FMT_ENABLE_IF(std::is_floating_point<T>::value)> 
1694 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, T value) { 
1695   if (const_check(!is_supported_floating_point(value))) return out; 
1696   auto fspecs = float_specs(); 
1697   if (std::signbit(value)) {  // value < 0 is false for NaN so use signbit. 
1698     fspecs.sign = sign::minus; 
1699     value = -value; 
1700   } 
1701  
1702   auto specs = basic_format_specs<Char>(); 
1703   if (!std::isfinite(value)) 
1704     return write_nonfinite(out, std::isinf(value), specs, fspecs); 
1705  
1706   memory_buffer buffer; 
1707   int precision = -1; 
1708   if (const_check(std::is_same<T, float>())) fspecs.binary32 = true; 
1709   fspecs.use_grisu = use_grisu<T>(); 
1710   int exp = format_float(promote_float(value), precision, fspecs, buffer); 
1711   fspecs.precision = precision; 
1712   float_writer<Char> w(buffer.data(), static_cast<int>(buffer.size()), exp, 
1713                        fspecs, static_cast<Char>('.')); 
1714   return base_iterator(out, w(reserve(out, w.size()))); 
1715 } 
1716  
1717 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt> 
1718 OutputIt write_char(OutputIt out, Char value, 
1719                     const basic_format_specs<Char>& specs) { 
1720   using iterator = remove_reference_t<decltype(reserve(out, 0))>; 
1721   return write_padded(out, specs, 1, [=](iterator it) { 
1722     *it++ = value; 
1723     return it; 
1724   }); 
1725 } 
1726  
1727 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt, typename UIntPtr> 
1728 OutputIt write_ptr(OutputIt out, UIntPtr value, 
1729                    const basic_format_specs<Char>* specs) { 
1730   int num_digits = count_digits<4>(value); 
1731   auto size = to_unsigned(num_digits) + size_t(2); 
1732   using iterator = remove_reference_t<decltype(reserve(out, 0))>; 
1733   auto write = [=](iterator it) { 
1734     *it++ = static_cast<Char>('0'); 
1735     *it++ = static_cast<Char>('x'); 
1736     return format_uint<4, Char>(it, value, num_digits); 
1737   }; 
1738   return specs ? write_padded<align::right>(out, *specs, size, write) 
1739                : base_iterator(out, write(reserve(out, size))); 
1740 }

1742 template <typename T> struct is_integral : std::is_integral<T> {}; 
1743 template <> struct is_integral<int128_t> : std::true_type {}; 
1744 template <> struct is_integral<uint128_t> : std::true_type {}; 
1745  
1746 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt> 
1747 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, monostate) { 
1748   FMT_ASSERT(false, ""); 
1749   return out; 
1750 }

1752 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt, 
1753           FMT_ENABLE_IF(!std::is_same<Char, char>::value)> 
1754 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, string_view value) { 
1755   auto it = reserve(out, value.size()); 
1756   it = copy_str<Char>(value.begin(), value.end(), it); 
1757   return base_iterator(out, it); 
1758 } 
1759  
1760 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt> 
1761 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, basic_string_view<Char> value) { 
1762   auto it = reserve(out, value.size()); 
1763   it = std::copy(value.begin(), value.end(), it); 
1764   return base_iterator(out, it); 
1765 } 
1766  
1767 template <typename Char> 
1768 buffer_appender<Char> write(buffer_appender<Char> out, 
1769                             basic_string_view<Char> value) { 
1770   get_container(out).append(value.begin(), value.end()); 
1771   return out; 
1772 } 
1773  
1774 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt, typename T, 
1775           FMT_ENABLE_IF(is_integral<T>::value && 
1776                         !std::is_same<T, bool>::value && 
1777                         !std::is_same<T, Char>::value)> 
1778 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, T value) { 
1779   auto abs_value = static_cast<uint32_or_64_or_128_t<T>>(value); 
1780   bool negative = is_negative(value); 
1781   // Don't do -abs_value since it trips unsigned-integer-overflow sanitizer. 
1782   if (negative) abs_value = ~abs_value + 1; 
1783   int num_digits = count_digits(abs_value); 
1784   auto size = (negative ? 1 : 0) + static_cast<size_t>(num_digits); 
1785   auto it = reserve(out, size); 
1786   if (auto ptr = to_pointer<Char>(it, size)) { 
1787     if (negative) *ptr++ = static_cast<Char>('-'); 
1788     format_decimal<Char>(ptr, abs_value, num_digits); 
1789     return out; 
1790   } 
1791   if (negative) *it++ = static_cast<Char>('-'); 
1792   it = format_decimal<Char>(it, abs_value, num_digits).end; 
1793   return base_iterator(out, it); 
1794 } 
1795  
1796 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt> 
1797 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, bool value) { 
1798   return write<Char>(out, string_view(value ? "true" : "false")); 
1799 } 
1800  
1801 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt> 
1802 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, Char value) { 
1803   auto it = reserve(out, 1); 
1804   *it++ = value; 
1805   return base_iterator(out, it); 
1806 } 
1807  
1808 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt> 
1809 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, const Char* value) { 
1810   if (!value) { 
1811     FMT_THROW(format_error("string pointer is null")); 
1812   } else { 
1813     auto length = std::char_traits<Char>::length(value); 
1814     out = write(out, basic_string_view<Char>(value, length)); 
1815   } 
1816   return out; 
1817 } 
1818  
1819 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt> 
1820 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, const void* value) { 
1821   return write_ptr<Char>(out, to_uintptr(value), nullptr); 
1822 } 
1823  
1824 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt, typename T> 
1825 auto write(OutputIt out, const T& value) -> typename std::enable_if< 
1826     mapped_type_constant<T, basic_format_context<OutputIt, Char>>::value == 
1827         type::custom_type, 
1828     OutputIt>::type { 
1829   basic_format_context<OutputIt, Char> ctx(out, {}, {}); 
1830   return formatter<T>().format(value, ctx); 
1831 } 
1832  
1833 // An argument visitor that formats the argument and writes it via the output 
1834 // iterator. It's a class and not a generic lambda for compatibility with C++11. 
1835 template <typename OutputIt, typename Char> struct default_arg_formatter { 
1836   using context = basic_format_context<OutputIt, Char>; 
1837  
1838   OutputIt out; 
1839   basic_format_args<context> args; 
1840   locale_ref loc; 
1841  
1842   template <typename T> OutputIt operator()(T value) { 
1843     return write<Char>(out, value); 
1844   } 
1845  
1846   OutputIt operator()(typename basic_format_arg<context>::handle handle) { 
1847     basic_format_parse_context<Char> parse_ctx({}); 
1848     basic_format_context<OutputIt, Char> format_ctx(out, args, loc); 
1849     handle.format(parse_ctx, format_ctx); 
1850     return format_ctx.out(); 
1851   } 
1852 }; 
1853  
1854 template <typename OutputIt, typename Char, 
1855           typename ErrorHandler = error_handler> 
1856 class arg_formatter_base { 
1857  public: 
1858   using iterator = OutputIt; 
1859   using char_type = Char; 
1860   using format_specs = basic_format_specs<Char>; 
1861  
1862  private: 
1863   iterator out_; 
1864   locale_ref locale_; 
1865   format_specs* specs_; 
1866  
1867   // Attempts to reserve space for n extra characters in the output range. 
1868   // Returns a pointer to the reserved range or a reference to out_. 
1869   auto reserve(size_t n) -> decltype(detail::reserve(out_, n)) { 
1870     return detail::reserve(out_, n); 
1871   } 
1872  
1873   using reserve_iterator = remove_reference_t<decltype( 
1874       detail::reserve(std::declval<iterator&>(), 0))>; 
1875  
1876   template <typename T> void write_int(T value, const format_specs& spec) { 
1877     using uint_type = uint32_or_64_or_128_t<T>; 
1878     int_writer<iterator, Char, uint_type> w(out_, locale_, value, spec); 
1879     handle_int_type_spec(spec.type, w); 
1880     out_ = w.out; 
1881   } 
1882  
1883   void write(char value) { 
1884     auto&& it = reserve(1); 
1885     *it++ = value; 
1886   } 
1887  
1888   template <typename Ch, FMT_ENABLE_IF(std::is_same<Ch, Char>::value)> 
1889   void write(Ch value) { 
1890     out_ = detail::write<Char>(out_, value); 
1891   } 
1892  
1893   void write(string_view value) { 
1894     auto&& it = reserve(value.size()); 
1895     it = copy_str<Char>(value.begin(), value.end(), it); 
1896   } 
1897   void write(wstring_view value) { 
1898     static_assert(std::is_same<Char, wchar_t>::value, ""); 
1899     auto&& it = reserve(value.size()); 
1900     it = std::copy(value.begin(), value.end(), it); 
1901   } 
1902  
1903   template <typename Ch> 
1904   void write(const Ch* s, size_t size, const format_specs& specs) { 
1905     auto width = specs.width != 0 
1906                      ? count_code_points(basic_string_view<Ch>(s, size)) 
1907                      : 0; 
1908     out_ = write_padded(out_, specs, size, width, [=](reserve_iterator it) { 
1909       return copy_str<Char>(s, s + size, it); 
1910     }); 
1911   } 
1912  
1913   template <typename Ch> 
1914   void write(basic_string_view<Ch> s, const format_specs& specs = {}) { 
1915     out_ = detail::write(out_, s, specs); 
1916   } 
1917  
1918   void write_pointer(const void* p) { 
1919     out_ = write_ptr<char_type>(out_, to_uintptr(p), specs_); 
1920   } 
1921  
1922   struct char_spec_handler : ErrorHandler { 
1923     arg_formatter_base& formatter; 
1924     Char value; 
1925  
1926     char_spec_handler(arg_formatter_base& f, Char val) 
1927         : formatter(f), value(val) {} 
1928  
1929     void on_int() { 
1930       // char is only formatted as int if there are specs. 
1931       formatter.write_int(static_cast<int>(value), *formatter.specs_); 
1932     } 
1933     void on_char() { 
1934       if (formatter.specs_) 
1935         formatter.out_ = write_char(formatter.out_, value, *formatter.specs_); 
1936       else 
1937         formatter.write(value); 
1938     } 
1939   }; 
1940  
1941   struct cstring_spec_handler : error_handler { 
1942     arg_formatter_base& formatter; 
1943     const Char* value; 
1944  
1945     cstring_spec_handler(arg_formatter_base& f, const Char* val) 
1946         : formatter(f), value(val) {} 
1947  
1948     void on_string() { formatter.write(value); } 
1949     void on_pointer() { formatter.write_pointer(value); } 
1950   }; 
1951  
1952  protected: 
1953   iterator out() { return out_; } 
1954   format_specs* specs() { return specs_; } 
1955  
1956   void write(bool value) { 
1957     if (specs_) 
1958       write(string_view(value ? "true" : "false"), *specs_); 
1959     else 
1960       out_ = detail::write<Char>(out_, value); 
1961   } 
1962  
1963   void write(const Char* value) { 
1964     if (!value) { 
1965       FMT_THROW(format_error("string pointer is null")); 
1966     } else { 
1967       auto length = std::char_traits<char_type>::length(value); 
1968       basic_string_view<char_type> sv(value, length); 
1969       specs_ ? write(sv, *specs_) : write(sv); 
1970     } 
1971   } 
1972  
1973  public: 
1974   arg_formatter_base(OutputIt out, format_specs* s, locale_ref loc) 
1975       : out_(out), locale_(loc), specs_(s) {} 
1976  
1977   iterator operator()(monostate) { 
1978     FMT_ASSERT(false, "invalid argument type"); 
1979     return out_; 
1980   } 
1981  
1982   template <typename T, FMT_ENABLE_IF(is_integral<T>::value)> 
1983   FMT_INLINE iterator operator()(T value) { 
1984     if (specs_) 
1985       write_int(value, *specs_); 
1986     else 
1987       out_ = detail::write<Char>(out_, value); 
1988     return out_; 
1989   } 
1990  
1991   iterator operator()(Char value) { 
1992     handle_char_specs(specs_, 
1993                       char_spec_handler(*this, static_cast<Char>(value))); 
1994     return out_; 
1995   } 
1996  
1997   iterator operator()(bool value) { 
1998     if (specs_ && specs_->type) return (*this)(value ? 1 : 0); 
1999     write(value != 0); 
2000     return out_; 
2001   } 
2002  
2003   template <typename T, FMT_ENABLE_IF(std::is_floating_point<T>::value)> 
2004   iterator operator()(T value) { 
2005     auto specs = specs_ ? *specs_ : format_specs(); 
2006     if (const_check(is_supported_floating_point(value))) 
2007       out_ = detail::write(out_, value, specs, locale_); 
2008     else 
2009       FMT_ASSERT(false, "unsupported float argument type"); 
2010     return out_; 
2011   } 
2012  
2013   iterator operator()(const Char* value) { 
2014     if (!specs_) return write(value), out_; 
2015     handle_cstring_type_spec(specs_->type, cstring_spec_handler(*this, value)); 
2016     return out_; 
2017   } 
2018  
2019   iterator operator()(basic_string_view<Char> value) { 
2020     if (specs_) { 
2021       check_string_type_spec(specs_->type, error_handler()); 
2022       write(value, *specs_); 
2023     } else { 
2024       write(value); 
2025     } 
2026     return out_; 
2027   } 
2028  
2029   iterator operator()(const void* value) { 
2030     if (specs_) check_pointer_type_spec(specs_->type, error_handler()); 
2031     write_pointer(value); 
2032     return out_; 
2033   } 
2034 }; 
2035  
2036 template <typename Char> FMT_CONSTEXPR bool is_name_start(Char c) { 
2037   return ('a' <= c && c <= 'z') || ('A' <= c && c <= 'Z') || '_' == c; 
2038 } 
2039  
2040 // Parses the range [begin, end) as an unsigned integer. This function assumes 
2041 // that the range is non-empty and the first character is a digit. 
2042 template <typename Char, typename ErrorHandler> 
2043 FMT_CONSTEXPR int parse_nonnegative_int(const Char*& begin, const Char* end, 
2044                                         ErrorHandler&& eh) { 
2045   FMT_ASSERT(begin != end && '0' <= *begin && *begin <= '9', ""); 
2046   unsigned value = 0; 
2047   // Convert to unsigned to prevent a warning. 
2048   constexpr unsigned max_int = max_value<int>(); 
2049   unsigned big = max_int / 10; 
2050   do { 
2051     // Check for overflow. 
2052     if (value > big) { 
2053       value = max_int + 1; 
2054       break; 
2055     } 
2056     value = value * 10 + unsigned(*begin - '0'); 
2057     ++begin; 
2058   } while (begin != end && '0' <= *begin && *begin <= '9'); 
2059   if (value > max_int) eh.on_error("number is too big"); 
2060   return static_cast<int>(value); 
2061 }

2063 template <typename Context> class custom_formatter { 
2064  private: 
2065   using char_type = typename Context::char_type; 
2066  
2067   basic_format_parse_context<char_type>& parse_ctx_; 
2068   Context& ctx_; 
2069  
2070  public: 
2071   explicit custom_formatter(basic_format_parse_context<char_type>& parse_ctx, 
2072                             Context& ctx) 
2073       : parse_ctx_(parse_ctx), ctx_(ctx) {} 
2074  
2075   bool operator()(typename basic_format_arg<Context>::handle h) const { 
2076     h.format(parse_ctx_, ctx_); 
2077     return true; 
2078   } 
2079  
2080   template <typename T> bool operator()(T) const { return false; } 
2081 };

2083 template <typename T> 
2084 using is_integer = 
2085     bool_constant<is_integral<T>::value && !std::is_same<T, bool>::value && 
2086                   !std::is_same<T, char>::value && 
2087                   !std::is_same<T, wchar_t>::value>;

2089 template <typename ErrorHandler> class width_checker { 
2090  public: 
2091   explicit FMT_CONSTEXPR width_checker(ErrorHandler& eh) : handler_(eh) {} 
2092  
2093   template <typename T, FMT_ENABLE_IF(is_integer<T>::value)> 
2094   FMT_CONSTEXPR unsigned long long operator()(T value) { 
2095     if (is_negative(value)) handler_.on_error("negative width"); 
2096     return static_cast<unsigned long long>(value); 
2097   } 
2098  
2099   template <typename T, FMT_ENABLE_IF(!is_integer<T>::value)> 
2100   FMT_CONSTEXPR unsigned long long operator()(T) { 
2101     handler_.on_error("width is not integer"); 
2102     return 0; 
2103   } 
2104  
2105  private: 
2106   ErrorHandler& handler_; 
2107 };

2109 template <typename ErrorHandler> class precision_checker { 
2110  public: 
2111   explicit FMT_CONSTEXPR precision_checker(ErrorHandler& eh) : handler_(eh) {} 
2112  
2113   template <typename T, FMT_ENABLE_IF(is_integer<T>::value)> 
2114   FMT_CONSTEXPR unsigned long long operator()(T value) { 
2115     if (is_negative(value)) handler_.on_error("negative precision"); 
2116     return static_cast<unsigned long long>(value); 
2117   } 
2118  
2119   template <typename T, FMT_ENABLE_IF(!is_integer<T>::value)> 
2120   FMT_CONSTEXPR unsigned long long operator()(T) { 
2121     handler_.on_error("precision is not integer"); 
2122     return 0; 
2123   } 
2124  
2125  private: 
2126   ErrorHandler& handler_; 
2127 };

2129 // A format specifier handler that sets fields in basic_format_specs. 
2130 template <typename Char> class specs_setter { 
2131  public: 
2132   explicit FMT_CONSTEXPR specs_setter(basic_format_specs<Char>& specs) 
2133       : specs_(specs) {} 
2134  
2135   FMT_CONSTEXPR specs_setter(const specs_setter& other) 
2136       : specs_(other.specs_) {} 
2137  
2138   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_align(align_t align) { specs_.align = align; } 
2139   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_fill(basic_string_view<Char> fill) { 
2140     specs_.fill = fill; 
2141   } 
2142   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_plus() { specs_.sign = sign::plus; } 
2143   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_minus() { specs_.sign = sign::minus; } 
2144   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_space() { specs_.sign = sign::space; } 
2145   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_hash() { specs_.alt = true; } 
2146  
2147   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_zero() { 
2148     specs_.align = align::numeric; 
2149     specs_.fill[0] = Char('0'); 
2150   } 
2151  
2152   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_width(int width) { specs_.width = width; } 
2153   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_precision(int precision) { 
2154     specs_.precision = precision; 
2155   } 
2156   FMT_CONSTEXPR void end_precision() {} 
2157  
2158   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_type(Char type) { 
2159     specs_.type = static_cast<char>(type); 
2160   } 
2161  
2162  protected: 
2163   basic_format_specs<Char>& specs_; 
2164 }; 
2165  
2166 template <typename ErrorHandler> class numeric_specs_checker { 
2167  public: 
2168   FMT_CONSTEXPR numeric_specs_checker(ErrorHandler& eh, detail::type arg_type) 
2169       : error_handler_(eh), arg_type_(arg_type) {} 
2170  
2171   FMT_CONSTEXPR void require_numeric_argument() { 
2172     if (!is_arithmetic_type(arg_type_)) 
2173       error_handler_.on_error("format specifier requires numeric argument"); 
2174   } 
2175  
2176   FMT_CONSTEXPR void check_sign() { 
2177     require_numeric_argument(); 
2178     if (is_integral_type(arg_type_) && arg_type_ != type::int_type && 
2179         arg_type_ != type::long_long_type && arg_type_ != type::char_type) { 
2180       error_handler_.on_error("format specifier requires signed argument"); 
2181     } 
2182   } 
2183  
2184   FMT_CONSTEXPR void check_precision() { 
2185     if (is_integral_type(arg_type_) || arg_type_ == type::pointer_type) 
2186       error_handler_.on_error("precision not allowed for this argument type"); 
2187   } 
2188  
2189  private: 
2190   ErrorHandler& error_handler_; 
2191   detail::type arg_type_; 
2192 };

2194 // A format specifier handler that checks if specifiers are consistent with the 
2195 // argument type. 
2196 template <typename Handler> class specs_checker : public Handler { 
2197  private: 
2198   numeric_specs_checker<Handler> checker_; 
2199  
2200   // Suppress an MSVC warning about using this in initializer list. 
2201   FMT_CONSTEXPR Handler& error_handler() { return *this; } 
2202  
2203  public: 
2204   FMT_CONSTEXPR specs_checker(const Handler& handler, detail::type arg_type) 
2205       : Handler(handler), checker_(error_handler(), arg_type) {} 
2206  
2207   FMT_CONSTEXPR specs_checker(const specs_checker& other) 
2208       : Handler(other), checker_(error_handler(), other.arg_type_) {} 
2209  
2210   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_align(align_t align) { 
2211     if (align == align::numeric) checker_.require_numeric_argument(); 
2212     Handler::on_align(align); 
2213   } 
2214  
2215   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_plus() { 
2216     checker_.check_sign(); 
2217     Handler::on_plus(); 
2218   } 
2219  
2220   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_minus() { 
2221     checker_.check_sign(); 
2222     Handler::on_minus(); 
2223   } 
2224  
2225   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_space() { 
2226     checker_.check_sign(); 
2227     Handler::on_space(); 
2228   } 
2229  
2230   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_hash() { 
2231     checker_.require_numeric_argument(); 
2232     Handler::on_hash(); 
2233   } 
2234  
2235   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_zero() { 
2236     checker_.require_numeric_argument(); 
2237     Handler::on_zero(); 
2238   } 
2239  
2240   FMT_CONSTEXPR void end_precision() { checker_.check_precision(); } 
2241 };

2243 template <template <typename> class Handler, typename FormatArg, 
2244           typename ErrorHandler> 
2245 FMT_CONSTEXPR int get_dynamic_spec(FormatArg arg, ErrorHandler eh) { 
2246   unsigned long long value = visit_format_arg(Handler<ErrorHandler>(eh), arg); 
2247   if (value > to_unsigned(max_value<int>())) eh.on_error("number is too big"); 
2248   return static_cast<int>(value); 
2249 }

2253 template <typename Context, typename ID> 
2254 FMT_CONSTEXPR typename Context::format_arg get_arg(Context& ctx, ID id) { 
2255   auto arg = ctx.arg(id); 
2256   if (!arg) ctx.on_error("argument not found"); 
2257   return arg; 
2258 }

2260 // The standard format specifier handler with checking. 
2261 template <typename ParseContext, typename Context> 
2262 class specs_handler : public specs_setter<typename Context::char_type> { 
2263  public: 
2264   using char_type = typename Context::char_type; 
2265  
2266   FMT_CONSTEXPR specs_handler(basic_format_specs<char_type>& specs, 
2267                               ParseContext& parse_ctx, Context& ctx) 
2268       : specs_setter<char_type>(specs), 
2269         parse_context_(parse_ctx), 
2270         context_(ctx) {} 
2271  
2272   template <typename Id> FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_dynamic_width(Id arg_id) { 
2273     this->specs_.width = get_dynamic_spec<width_checker>( 
2274         get_arg(arg_id), context_.error_handler()); 
2275   } 
2276  
2277   template <typename Id> FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_dynamic_precision(Id arg_id) { 
2278     this->specs_.precision = get_dynamic_spec<precision_checker>( 
2279         get_arg(arg_id), context_.error_handler()); 
2280   } 
2281  
2282   void on_error(const char* message) { context_.on_error(message); } 
2283  
2284  private: 
2285   // This is only needed for compatibility with gcc 4.4. 
2286   using format_arg = typename Context::format_arg; 
2287  
2288   FMT_CONSTEXPR format_arg get_arg(auto_id) { 
2289     return detail::get_arg(context_, parse_context_.next_arg_id()); 
2290   } 
2291  
2292   FMT_CONSTEXPR format_arg get_arg(int arg_id) { 
2293     parse_context_.check_arg_id(arg_id); 
2294     return detail::get_arg(context_, arg_id); 
2295   } 
2296  
2297   FMT_CONSTEXPR format_arg get_arg(basic_string_view<char_type> arg_id) { 
2298     parse_context_.check_arg_id(arg_id); 
2299     return detail::get_arg(context_, arg_id); 
2300   } 
2301  
2302   ParseContext& parse_context_; 
2303   Context& context_; 
2304 };
