 898 // Compares two characters for equality. 
 899 template <typename Char> bool equal2(const Char* lhs, const char* rhs) { 
 900   return lhs[0] == rhs[0] && lhs[1] == rhs[1]; 
 901 } 
 902 inline bool equal2(const char* lhs, const char* rhs) { 
 903   return memcmp(lhs, rhs, 2) == 0; 
 904 } 
 905  
 906 // Copies two characters from src to dst. 
 907 template <typename Char> void copy2(Char* dst, const char* src) { 
 908   *dst++ = static_cast<Char>(*src++); 
 909   *dst = static_cast<Char>(*src); 
 910 } 
 911 inline void copy2(char* dst, const char* src) { memcpy(dst, src, 2); } 
 912  
 913 template <typename Iterator> struct format_decimal_result { 
 914   Iterator begin; 
 915   Iterator end; 
 916 }; 
 917  
 918 // Formats a decimal unsigned integer value writing into out pointing to a 
 919 // buffer of specified size. The caller must ensure that the buffer is large 
 920 // enough. 
 921 template <typename Char, typename UInt> 
 922 inline format_decimal_result<Char*> format_decimal(Char* out, UInt value, 
 923                                                    int size) { 
 924   FMT_ASSERT(size >= count_digits(value), "invalid digit count"); 
 925   out += size; 
 926   Char* end = out; 
 927   while (value >= 100) { 
 928     // Integer division is slow so do it for a group of two digits instead 
 929     // of for every digit. The idea comes from the talk by Alexandrescu 
 930     // "Three Optimization Tips for C++". See speed-test for a comparison. 
 931     out -= 2; 
 932     copy2(out, data::digits[value % 100]); 
 933     value /= 100; 
 934   } 
 935   if (value < 10) { 
 936     *--out = static_cast<Char>('0' + value); 
 937     return {out, end}; 
 938   } 
 939   out -= 2; 
 940   copy2(out, data::digits[value]); 
 941   return {out, end}; 
 942 } 
 943  
 944 template <typename Char, typename UInt, typename Iterator, 
 945           FMT_ENABLE_IF(!std::is_pointer<remove_cvref_t<Iterator>>::value)> 
 946 inline format_decimal_result<Iterator> format_decimal(Iterator out, UInt value, 
 947                                                       int num_digits) { 
 948   // Buffer should be large enough to hold all digits (<= digits10 + 1). 
 949   enum { max_size = digits10<UInt>() + 1 }; 
 950   Char buffer[2 * max_size]; 
 951   auto end = format_decimal(buffer, value, num_digits).end; 
 952   return {out, detail::copy_str<Char>(buffer, end, out)}; 
 953 } 
 954  
 955 template <unsigned BASE_BITS, typename Char, typename UInt> 
 956 inline Char* format_uint(Char* buffer, UInt value, int num_digits, 
 957                          bool upper = false) { 
 958   buffer += num_digits; 
 959   Char* end = buffer; 
 960   do { 
 961     const char* digits = upper ? "0123456789ABCDEF" : data::hex_digits; 
 962     unsigned digit = (value & ((1 << BASE_BITS) - 1)); 
 963     *--buffer = static_cast<Char>(BASE_BITS < 4 ? static_cast<char>('0' + digit) 
 964                                                 : digits[digit]); 
 965   } while ((value >>= BASE_BITS) != 0); 
 966   return end; 
 967 } 
 968  
 969 template <unsigned BASE_BITS, typename Char> 
 970 Char* format_uint(Char* buffer, detail::fallback_uintptr n, int num_digits, 
 971                   bool = false) { 
 972   auto char_digits = std::numeric_limits<unsigned char>::digits / 4; 
 973   int start = (num_digits + char_digits - 1) / char_digits - 1; 
 974   if (int start_digits = num_digits % char_digits) { 
 975     unsigned value = n.value[start--]; 
 976     buffer = format_uint<BASE_BITS>(buffer, value, start_digits); 
 977   } 
 978   for (; start >= 0; --start) { 
 979     unsigned value = n.value[start]; 
 980     buffer += char_digits; 
 981     auto p = buffer; 
 982     for (int i = 0; i < char_digits; ++i) { 
 983       unsigned digit = (value & ((1 << BASE_BITS) - 1)); 
 984       *--p = static_cast<Char>(data::hex_digits[digit]); 
 985       value >>= BASE_BITS; 
 986     } 
 987   } 
 988   return buffer; 
 989 } 
 990  
 991 template <unsigned BASE_BITS, typename Char, typename It, typename UInt> 
 992 inline It format_uint(It out, UInt value, int num_digits, bool upper = false) { 
 993   // Buffer should be large enough to hold all digits (digits / BASE_BITS + 1). 
 994   char buffer[num_bits<UInt>() / BASE_BITS + 1]; 
 995   format_uint<BASE_BITS>(buffer, value, num_digits, upper); 
 996   return detail::copy_str<Char>(buffer, buffer + num_digits, out); 
 997 }

 999 // A converter from UTF-8 to UTF-16. 
1000 class utf8_to_utf16 { 
1001  private: 
1002   wmemory_buffer buffer_; 
1003  
1004  public: 
1005   FMT_API explicit utf8_to_utf16(string_view s); 
1006   operator wstring_view() const { return {&buffer_[0], size()}; } 
1007   size_t size() const { return buffer_.size() - 1; } 
1008   const wchar_t* c_str() const { return &buffer_[0]; } 
1009   std::wstring str() const { return {&buffer_[0], size()}; } 
1010 };

1012 template <typename T = void> struct null {};

1014 // Workaround an array initialization issue in gcc 4.8. 
1015 template <typename Char> struct fill_t { 
1016  private: 
1017   enum { max_size = 4 }; 
1018   Char data_[max_size]; 
1019   unsigned char size_; 
1020  
1021  public: 
1022   FMT_CONSTEXPR void operator=(basic_string_view<Char> s) { 
1023     auto size = s.size(); 
1024     if (size > max_size) { 
1025       FMT_THROW(format_error("invalid fill")); 
1026       return; 
1027     } 
1028     for (size_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) data_[i] = s[i]; 
1029     size_ = static_cast<unsigned char>(size); 
1030   } 
1031  
1032   size_t size() const { return size_; } 
1033   const Char* data() const { return data_; } 
1034  
1035   FMT_CONSTEXPR Char& operator[](size_t index) { return data_[index]; } 
1036   FMT_CONSTEXPR const Char& operator[](size_t index) const { 
1037     return data_[index]; 
1038   } 
1039  
1040   static FMT_CONSTEXPR fill_t<Char> make() { 
1041     auto fill = fill_t<Char>(); 
1042     fill[0] = Char(' '); 
1043     fill.size_ = 1; 
1044     return fill; 
1045   } 
1046 }; 
1047 }  // namespace detail

1063   int width; 
1064   int precision; 
1065   char type; 
1066   align_t align : 4; 
1067   sign_t sign : 3; 
1068   bool alt : 1;  // Alternate form ('#'). 
1069   detail::fill_t<Char> fill; 
1070  
1071   constexpr basic_format_specs() 
1072       : width(0), 
1073         precision(-1), 
1074         type(0), 
1075         align(align::none), 
1076         sign(sign::none), 
1077         alt(false), 
1078         fill(detail::fill_t<Char>::make()) {} 
1079 };

1085 // A floating-point presentation format. 
1086 enum class float_format : unsigned char { 
1087   general,  // General: exponent notation or fixed point based on magnitude. 
1088   exp,      // Exponent notation with the default precision of 6, e.g. 1.2e-3. 
1089   fixed,    // Fixed point with the default precision of 6, e.g. 0.0012. 
1090   hex 
1091 }; 
1092  
1093 struct float_specs { 
1094   int precision; 
1095   float_format format : 8; 
1096   sign_t sign : 8; 
1097   bool upper : 1; 
1098   bool locale : 1; 
1099   bool binary32 : 1; 
1100   bool use_grisu : 1; 
1101   bool showpoint : 1; 
1102 }; 
1103  
1104 // Writes the exponent exp in the form "[+-]d{2,3}" to buffer. 
1105 template <typename Char, typename It> It write_exponent(int exp, It it) { 
1106   FMT_ASSERT(-10000 < exp && exp < 10000, "exponent out of range"); 
1107   if (exp < 0) { 
1108     *it++ = static_cast<Char>('-'); 
1109     exp = -exp; 
1110   } else { 
1111     *it++ = static_cast<Char>('+'); 
1112   } 
1113   if (exp >= 100) { 
1114     const char* top = data::digits[exp / 100]; 
1115     if (exp >= 1000) *it++ = static_cast<Char>(top[0]); 
1116     *it++ = static_cast<Char>(top[1]); 
1117     exp %= 100; 
1118   } 
1119   const char* d = data::digits[exp]; 
1120   *it++ = static_cast<Char>(d[0]); 
1121   *it++ = static_cast<Char>(d[1]); 
1122   return it; 
1123 } 
1124  
1125 template <typename Char> class float_writer { 
1126  private: 
1127   // The number is given as v = digits_ * pow(10, exp_). 
1128   const char* digits_; 
1129   int num_digits_; 
1130   int exp_; 
1131   size_t size_; 
1132   float_specs specs_; 
1133   Char decimal_point_; 
1134  
1135   template <typename It> It prettify(It it) const { 
1136     // pow(10, full_exp - 1) <= v <= pow(10, full_exp). 
1137     int full_exp = num_digits_ + exp_; 
1138     if (specs_.format == float_format::exp) { 
1139       // Insert a decimal point after the first digit and add an exponent. 
1140       *it++ = static_cast<Char>(*digits_); 
1141       int num_zeros = specs_.precision - num_digits_; 
1142       if (num_digits_ > 1 || specs_.showpoint) *it++ = decimal_point_; 
1143       it = copy_str<Char>(digits_ + 1, digits_ + num_digits_, it); 
1144       if (num_zeros > 0 && specs_.showpoint) 
1145         it = std::fill_n(it, num_zeros, static_cast<Char>('0')); 
1146       *it++ = static_cast<Char>(specs_.upper ? 'E' : 'e'); 
1147       return write_exponent<Char>(full_exp - 1, it); 
1148     } 
1149     if (num_digits_ <= full_exp) { 
1150       // 1234e7 -> 12340000000[.0+] 
1151       it = copy_str<Char>(digits_, digits_ + num_digits_, it); 
1152       it = std::fill_n(it, full_exp - num_digits_, static_cast<Char>('0')); 
1153       if (specs_.showpoint || specs_.precision < 0) { 
1154         *it++ = decimal_point_; 
1155         int num_zeros = specs_.precision - full_exp; 
1156         if (num_zeros <= 0) { 
1157           if (specs_.format != float_format::fixed) 
1158             *it++ = static_cast<Char>('0'); 
1159           return it; 
1160         } 
1161 #ifdef FMT_FUZZ 
1162         if (num_zeros > 5000) 
1163           throw std::runtime_error("fuzz mode - avoiding excessive cpu use"); 
1164 #endif 
1165         it = std::fill_n(it, num_zeros, static_cast<Char>('0')); 
1166       } 
1167     } else if (full_exp > 0) { 
1168       // 1234e-2 -> 12.34[0+] 
1169       it = copy_str<Char>(digits_, digits_ + full_exp, it); 
1170       if (!specs_.showpoint) { 
1171         // Remove trailing zeros. 
1172         int num_digits = num_digits_; 
1173         while (num_digits > full_exp && digits_[num_digits - 1] == '0') 
1174           --num_digits; 
1175         if (num_digits != full_exp) *it++ = decimal_point_; 
1176         return copy_str<Char>(digits_ + full_exp, digits_ + num_digits, it); 
1177       } 
1178       *it++ = decimal_point_; 
1179       it = copy_str<Char>(digits_ + full_exp, digits_ + num_digits_, it); 
1180       if (specs_.precision > num_digits_) { 
1181         // Add trailing zeros. 
1182         int num_zeros = specs_.precision - num_digits_; 
1183         it = std::fill_n(it, num_zeros, static_cast<Char>('0')); 
1184       } 
1185     } else { 
1186       // 1234e-6 -> 0.001234 
1187       *it++ = static_cast<Char>('0'); 
1188       int num_zeros = -full_exp; 
1189       int num_digits = num_digits_; 
1190       if (num_digits == 0 && specs_.precision >= 0 && 
1191           specs_.precision < num_zeros) { 
1192         num_zeros = specs_.precision; 
1193       } 
1194       // Remove trailing zeros. 
1195       if (!specs_.showpoint) 
1196         while (num_digits > 0 && digits_[num_digits - 1] == '0') --num_digits; 
1197       if (num_zeros != 0 || num_digits != 0 || specs_.showpoint) { 
1198         *it++ = decimal_point_; 
1199         it = std::fill_n(it, num_zeros, static_cast<Char>('0')); 
1200         it = copy_str<Char>(digits_, digits_ + num_digits, it); 
1201       } 
1202     } 
1203     return it; 
1204   } 
1205  
1206  public: 
1207   float_writer(const char* digits, int num_digits, int exp, float_specs specs, 
1208                Char decimal_point) 
1209       : digits_(digits), 
1210         num_digits_(num_digits), 
1211         exp_(exp), 
1212         specs_(specs), 
1213         decimal_point_(decimal_point) { 
1214     int full_exp = num_digits + exp - 1; 
1215     int precision = specs.precision > 0 ? specs.precision : 16; 
1216     if (specs_.format == float_format::general && 
1217         !(full_exp >= -4 && full_exp < precision)) { 
1218       specs_.format = float_format::exp; 
1219     } 
1220     size_ = prettify(counting_iterator()).count(); 
1221     size_ += specs.sign ? 1 : 0; 
1222   } 
1223  
1224   size_t size() const { return size_; } 
1225  
1226   template <typename It> It operator()(It it) const { 
1227     if (specs_.sign) *it++ = static_cast<Char>(data::signs[specs_.sign]); 
1228     return prettify(it); 
1229   } 
1230 }; 
1231  
1232 template <typename T> 
1233 int format_float(T value, int precision, float_specs specs, buffer<char>& buf); 
1234  
1235 // Formats a floating-point number with snprintf. 
1236 template <typename T> 
1237 int snprintf_float(T value, int precision, float_specs specs, 
1238                    buffer<char>& buf); 
1239  
1240 template <typename T> T promote_float(T value) { return value; } 
1241 inline double promote_float(float value) { return static_cast<double>(value); } 
1242  
1243 template <typename Handler> 
1244 FMT_CONSTEXPR void handle_int_type_spec(char spec, Handler&& handler) { 
1245   switch (spec) { 
1246   case 0: 
1247   case 'd': 
1248     handler.on_dec(); 
1249     break; 
1250   case 'x': 
1251   case 'X': 
1252     handler.on_hex(); 
1253     break; 
1254   case 'b': 
1255   case 'B': 
1256     handler.on_bin(); 
1257     break; 
1258   case 'o': 
1259     handler.on_oct(); 
1260     break; 
1261 #ifdef FMT_DEPRECATED_N_SPECIFIER 
1262   case 'n': 
1263 #endif 
1264   case 'L': 
1265     handler.on_num(); 
1266     break; 
1267   case 'c': 
1268     handler.on_chr(); 
1269     break; 
1270   default: 
1271     handler.on_error(); 
1272   } 
1273 } 
1274  
1275 template <typename ErrorHandler = error_handler, typename Char> 
1276 FMT_CONSTEXPR float_specs parse_float_type_spec( 
1277     const basic_format_specs<Char>& specs, ErrorHandler&& eh = {}) { 
1278   auto result = float_specs(); 
1279   result.showpoint = specs.alt; 
1280   switch (specs.type) { 
1281   case 0: 
1282     result.format = float_format::general; 
1283     result.showpoint |= specs.precision > 0; 
1284     break; 
1285   case 'G': 
1286     result.upper = true; 
1287     FMT_FALLTHROUGH; 
1288   case 'g': 
1289     result.format = float_format::general; 
1290     break; 
1291   case 'E': 
1292     result.upper = true; 
1293     FMT_FALLTHROUGH; 
1294   case 'e': 
1295     result.format = float_format::exp; 
1296     result.showpoint |= specs.precision != 0; 
1297     break; 
1298   case 'F': 
1299     result.upper = true; 
1300     FMT_FALLTHROUGH; 
1301   case 'f': 
1302     result.format = float_format::fixed; 
1303     result.showpoint |= specs.precision != 0; 
1304     break; 
1305   case 'A': 
1306     result.upper = true; 
1307     FMT_FALLTHROUGH; 
1308   case 'a': 
1309     result.format = float_format::hex; 
1310     break; 
1311 #ifdef FMT_DEPRECATED_N_SPECIFIER 
1312   case 'n': 
1313 #endif 
1314   case 'L': 
1315     result.locale = true; 
1316     break; 
1317   default: 
1318     eh.on_error("invalid type specifier"); 
1319     break; 
1320   } 
1321   return result; 
1322 } 
1323  
1324 template <typename Char, typename Handler> 
1325 FMT_CONSTEXPR void handle_char_specs(const basic_format_specs<Char>* specs, 
1326                                      Handler&& handler) { 
1327   if (!specs) return handler.on_char(); 
1328   if (specs->type && specs->type != 'c') return handler.on_int(); 
1329   if (specs->align == align::numeric || specs->sign != sign::none || specs->alt) 
1330     handler.on_error("invalid format specifier for char"); 
1331   handler.on_char(); 
1332 } 
1333  
1334 template <typename Char, typename Handler> 
1335 FMT_CONSTEXPR void handle_cstring_type_spec(Char spec, Handler&& handler) { 
1336   if (spec == 0 || spec == 's') 
1337     handler.on_string(); 
1338   else if (spec == 'p') 
1339     handler.on_pointer(); 
1340   else 
1341     handler.on_error("invalid type specifier"); 
1342 } 
1343  
1344 template <typename Char, typename ErrorHandler> 
1345 FMT_CONSTEXPR void check_string_type_spec(Char spec, ErrorHandler&& eh) { 
1346   if (spec != 0 && spec != 's') eh.on_error("invalid type specifier"); 
1347 } 
1348  
1349 template <typename Char, typename ErrorHandler> 
1350 FMT_CONSTEXPR void check_pointer_type_spec(Char spec, ErrorHandler&& eh) { 
1351   if (spec != 0 && spec != 'p') eh.on_error("invalid type specifier"); 
1352 }

1354 template <typename ErrorHandler> class int_type_checker : private ErrorHandler { 
1355  public: 
1356   FMT_CONSTEXPR explicit int_type_checker(ErrorHandler eh) : ErrorHandler(eh) {} 
1357  
1358   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_dec() {} 
1359   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_hex() {} 
1360   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_bin() {} 
1361   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_oct() {} 
1362   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_num() {} 
1363   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_chr() {} 
1364  
1365   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_error() { 
1366     ErrorHandler::on_error("invalid type specifier"); 
1367   } 
1368 };

1370 template <typename ErrorHandler> 
1371 class char_specs_checker : public ErrorHandler { 
1372  private: 
1373   char type_; 
1374  
1375  public: 
1376   FMT_CONSTEXPR char_specs_checker(char type, ErrorHandler eh) 
1377       : ErrorHandler(eh), type_(type) {} 
1378  
1379   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_int() { 
1380     handle_int_type_spec(type_, int_type_checker<ErrorHandler>(*this)); 
1381   } 
1382   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_char() {} 
1383 };

1386 class cstring_type_checker : public ErrorHandler {

1388   FMT_CONSTEXPR explicit cstring_type_checker(ErrorHandler eh) 
1389       : ErrorHandler(eh) {} 
1390

1392   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_pointer() {}

1394  
1395 template <typename OutputIt, typename Char> 
1396 FMT_NOINLINE OutputIt fill(OutputIt it, size_t n, const fill_t<Char>& fill) { 
1397   auto fill_size = fill.size(); 
1398   if (fill_size == 1) return std::fill_n(it, n, fill[0]); 
1399   for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) it = std::copy_n(fill.data(), fill_size, it); 
1400   return it; 
1401 } 
1402  
1403 // Writes the output of f, padded according to format specifications in specs. 
1404 // size: output size in code units. 
1405 // width: output display width in (terminal) column positions. 
1406 template <align::type align = align::left, typename OutputIt, typename Char, 
1407           typename F> 
1408 inline OutputIt write_padded(OutputIt out, 
1409                              const basic_format_specs<Char>& specs, size_t size, 
1410                              size_t width, const F& f) { 
1411   static_assert(align == align::left || align == align::right, ""); 
1412   unsigned spec_width = to_unsigned(specs.width); 
1413   size_t padding = spec_width > width ? spec_width - width : 0; 
1414   auto* shifts = align == align::left ? data::left_padding_shifts 
1415                                       : data::right_padding_shifts; 
1416   size_t left_padding = padding >> shifts[specs.align]; 
1417   auto it = reserve(out, size + padding * specs.fill.size()); 
1418   it = fill(it, left_padding, specs.fill); 
1419   it = f(it); 
1420   it = fill(it, padding - left_padding, specs.fill); 
1421   return base_iterator(out, it); 
1422 } 
1423  
1424 template <align::type align = align::left, typename OutputIt, typename Char, 
1425           typename F> 
1426 inline OutputIt write_padded(OutputIt out, 
1427                              const basic_format_specs<Char>& specs, size_t size, 
1428                              const F& f) { 
1429   return write_padded<align>(out, specs, size, size, f); 
1430 } 
1431  
1432 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt> 
1433 OutputIt write_bytes(OutputIt out, string_view bytes, 
1434                      const basic_format_specs<Char>& specs) { 
1435   using iterator = remove_reference_t<decltype(reserve(out, 0))>; 
1436   return write_padded(out, specs, bytes.size(), [bytes](iterator it) { 
1437     const char* data = bytes.data(); 
1438     return copy_str<Char>(data, data + bytes.size(), it); 
1439   }); 
1440 } 
1441  
1442 // Data for write_int that doesn't depend on output iterator type. It is used to 
1443 // avoid template code bloat. 
1444 template <typename Char> struct write_int_data { 
1445   size_t size; 
1446   size_t padding; 
1447  
1448   write_int_data(int num_digits, string_view prefix, 
1449                  const basic_format_specs<Char>& specs) 
1450       : size(prefix.size() + to_unsigned(num_digits)), padding(0) { 
1451     if (specs.align == align::numeric) { 
1452       auto width = to_unsigned(specs.width); 
1453       if (width > size) { 
1454         padding = width - size; 
1455         size = width; 
1456       } 
1457     } else if (specs.precision > num_digits) { 
1458       size = prefix.size() + to_unsigned(specs.precision); 
1459       padding = to_unsigned(specs.precision - num_digits); 
1460     } 
1461   } 
1462 }; 
1463  
1464 // Writes an integer in the format 
1465 //   <left-padding><prefix><numeric-padding><digits><right-padding> 
1466 // where <digits> are written by f(it). 
1467 template <typename OutputIt, typename Char, typename F> 
1468 OutputIt write_int(OutputIt out, int num_digits, string_view prefix, 
1469                    const basic_format_specs<Char>& specs, F f) { 
1470   auto data = write_int_data<Char>(num_digits, prefix, specs); 
1471   using iterator = remove_reference_t<decltype(reserve(out, 0))>; 
1472   return write_padded<align::right>(out, specs, data.size, [=](iterator it) { 
1473     if (prefix.size() != 0) 
1474       it = copy_str<Char>(prefix.begin(), prefix.end(), it); 
1475     it = std::fill_n(it, data.padding, static_cast<Char>('0')); 
1476     return f(it); 
1477   }); 
1478 } 
1479  
1480 template <typename StrChar, typename Char, typename OutputIt> 
1481 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, basic_string_view<StrChar> s, 
1482                const basic_format_specs<Char>& specs) { 
1483   auto data = s.data(); 
1484   auto size = s.size(); 
1485   if (specs.precision >= 0 && to_unsigned(specs.precision) < size) 
1486     size = code_point_index(s, to_unsigned(specs.precision)); 
1487   auto width = specs.width != 0 
1488                    ? count_code_points(basic_string_view<StrChar>(data, size)) 
1489                    : 0; 
1490   using iterator = remove_reference_t<decltype(reserve(out, 0))>; 
1491   return write_padded(out, specs, size, width, [=](iterator it) { 
1492     return copy_str<Char>(data, data + size, it); 
1493   }); 
1494 } 
1495  
1496 // The handle_int_type_spec handler that writes an integer. 
1497 template <typename OutputIt, typename Char, typename UInt> struct int_writer { 
1498   OutputIt out; 
1499   locale_ref locale; 
1500   const basic_format_specs<Char>& specs; 
1501   UInt abs_value; 
1502   char prefix[4]; 
1503   unsigned prefix_size; 
1504  
1505   using iterator = 
1506       remove_reference_t<decltype(reserve(std::declval<OutputIt&>(), 0))>; 
1507  
1508   string_view get_prefix() const { return string_view(prefix, prefix_size); } 
1509  
1510   template <typename Int> 
1511   int_writer(OutputIt output, locale_ref loc, Int value, 
1512              const basic_format_specs<Char>& s) 
1513       : out(output), 
1514         locale(loc), 
1515         specs(s), 
1516         abs_value(static_cast<UInt>(value)), 
1517         prefix_size(0) { 
1518     static_assert(std::is_same<uint32_or_64_or_128_t<Int>, UInt>::value, ""); 
1519     if (is_negative(value)) { 
1520       prefix[0] = '-'; 
1521       ++prefix_size; 
1522       abs_value = 0 - abs_value; 
1523     } else if (specs.sign != sign::none && specs.sign != sign::minus) { 
1524       prefix[0] = specs.sign == sign::plus ? '+' : ' '; 
1525       ++prefix_size; 
1526     } 
1527   } 
1528  
1529   void on_dec() { 
1530     auto num_digits = count_digits(abs_value); 
1531     out = write_int( 
1532         out, num_digits, get_prefix(), specs, [this, num_digits](iterator it) { 
1533           return format_decimal<Char>(it, abs_value, num_digits).end; 
1534         }); 
1535   } 
1536  
1537   void on_hex() { 
1538     if (specs.alt) { 
1539       prefix[prefix_size++] = '0'; 
1540       prefix[prefix_size++] = specs.type; 
1541     } 
1542     int num_digits = count_digits<4>(abs_value); 
1543     out = write_int(out, num_digits, get_prefix(), specs, 
1544                     [this, num_digits](iterator it) { 
1545                       return format_uint<4, Char>(it, abs_value, num_digits, 
1546                                                   specs.type != 'x'); 
1547                     }); 
1548   } 
1549  
1550   void on_bin() { 
1551     if (specs.alt) { 
1552       prefix[prefix_size++] = '0'; 
1553       prefix[prefix_size++] = static_cast<char>(specs.type); 
1554     } 
1555     int num_digits = count_digits<1>(abs_value); 
1556     out = write_int(out, num_digits, get_prefix(), specs, 
1557                     [this, num_digits](iterator it) { 
1558                       return format_uint<1, Char>(it, abs_value, num_digits); 
1559                     }); 
1560   } 
1561  
1562   void on_oct() { 
1563     int num_digits = count_digits<3>(abs_value); 
1564     if (specs.alt && specs.precision <= num_digits && abs_value != 0) { 
1565       // Octal prefix '0' is counted as a digit, so only add it if precision 
1566       // is not greater than the number of digits. 
1567       prefix[prefix_size++] = '0'; 
1568     } 
1569     out = write_int(out, num_digits, get_prefix(), specs, 
1570                     [this, num_digits](iterator it) { 
1571                       return format_uint<3, Char>(it, abs_value, num_digits); 
1572                     }); 
1573   } 
1574  
1575   enum { sep_size = 1 }; 
1576  
1577   void on_num() { 
1578     std::string groups = grouping<Char>(locale); 
1579     if (groups.empty()) return on_dec(); 
1580     auto sep = thousands_sep<Char>(locale); 
1581     if (!sep) return on_dec(); 
1582     int num_digits = count_digits(abs_value); 
1583     int size = num_digits, n = num_digits; 
1584     std::string::const_iterator group = groups.cbegin(); 
1585     while (group != groups.cend() && n > *group && *group > 0 && 
1586            *group != max_value<char>()) { 
1587       size += sep_size; 
1588       n -= *group; 
1589       ++group; 
1590     } 
1591     if (group == groups.cend()) size += sep_size * ((n - 1) / groups.back()); 
1592     char digits[40]; 
1593     format_decimal(digits, abs_value, num_digits); 
1594     basic_memory_buffer<Char> buffer; 
1595     size += prefix_size; 
1596     buffer.resize(size); 
1597     basic_string_view<Char> s(&sep, sep_size); 
1598     // Index of a decimal digit with the least significant digit having index 0. 
1599     int digit_index = 0; 
1600     group = groups.cbegin(); 
1601     auto p = buffer.data() + size; 
1602     for (int i = num_digits - 1; i >= 0; --i) { 
1603       *--p = static_cast<Char>(digits[i]); 
1604       if (*group <= 0 || ++digit_index % *group != 0 || 
1605           *group == max_value<char>()) 
1606         continue; 
1607       if (group + 1 != groups.cend()) { 
1608         digit_index = 0; 
1609         ++group; 
1610       } 
1611       p -= s.size(); 
1612       std::uninitialized_copy(s.data(), s.data() + s.size(), 
1613                               make_checked(p, s.size())); 
1614     } 
1615     if (prefix_size != 0) p[-1] = static_cast<Char>('-'); 
1616     using iterator = remove_reference_t<decltype(reserve(out, 0))>; 
1617     auto data = buffer.data(); 
1618     out = write_padded<align::right>(out, specs, size, size, [=](iterator it) { 
1619       return copy_str<Char>(data, data + size, it); 
1620     }); 
1621   } 
1622  
1623   void on_chr() { *out++ = static_cast<Char>(abs_value); } 
1624  
1625   FMT_NORETURN void on_error() { 
1626     FMT_THROW(format_error("invalid type specifier")); 
1627   } 
1628 }; 
1629  
1630 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt> 
1631 OutputIt write_nonfinite(OutputIt out, bool isinf, 
1632                          const basic_format_specs<Char>& specs, 
1633                          const float_specs& fspecs) { 
1634   auto str = 
1635       isinf ? (fspecs.upper ? "INF" : "inf") : (fspecs.upper ? "NAN" : "nan"); 
1636   constexpr size_t str_size = 3; 
1637   auto sign = fspecs.sign; 
1638   auto size = str_size + (sign ? 1 : 0); 
1639   using iterator = remove_reference_t<decltype(reserve(out, 0))>; 
1640   return write_padded(out, specs, size, [=](iterator it) { 
1641     if (sign) *it++ = static_cast<Char>(data::signs[sign]); 
1642     return copy_str<Char>(str, str + str_size, it); 
1643   }); 
1644 } 
1645  
1646 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt, typename T, 
1647           FMT_ENABLE_IF(std::is_floating_point<T>::value)> 
1648 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, T value, basic_format_specs<Char> specs, 
1649                locale_ref loc = {}) { 
1650   if (const_check(!is_supported_floating_point(value))) return out; 
1651   float_specs fspecs = parse_float_type_spec(specs); 
1652   fspecs.sign = specs.sign; 
1653   if (std::signbit(value)) {  // value < 0 is false for NaN so use signbit. 
1654     fspecs.sign = sign::minus; 
1655     value = -value; 
1656   } else if (fspecs.sign == sign::minus) { 
1657     fspecs.sign = sign::none; 
1658   } 
1659  
1660   if (!std::isfinite(value)) 
1661     return write_nonfinite(out, std::isinf(value), specs, fspecs); 
1662  
1663   if (specs.align == align::numeric && fspecs.sign) { 
1664     auto it = reserve(out, 1); 
1665     *it++ = static_cast<Char>(data::signs[fspecs.sign]); 
1666     out = base_iterator(out, it); 
1667     fspecs.sign = sign::none; 
1668     if (specs.width != 0) --specs.width; 
1669   } 
1670  
1671   memory_buffer buffer; 
1672   if (fspecs.format == float_format::hex) { 
1673     if (fspecs.sign) buffer.push_back(data::signs[fspecs.sign]); 
1674     snprintf_float(promote_float(value), specs.precision, fspecs, buffer); 
1675     return write_bytes(out, {buffer.data(), buffer.size()}, specs); 
1676   } 
1677   int precision = specs.precision >= 0 || !specs.type ? specs.precision : 6; 
1678   if (fspecs.format == float_format::exp) { 
1679     if (precision == max_value<int>()) 
1680       FMT_THROW(format_error("number is too big")); 
1681     else 
1682       ++precision; 
1683   } 
1684   if (const_check(std::is_same<T, float>())) fspecs.binary32 = true; 
1685   fspecs.use_grisu = use_grisu<T>(); 
1686   int exp = format_float(promote_float(value), precision, fspecs, buffer); 
1687   fspecs.precision = precision; 
1688   Char point = 
1689       fspecs.locale ? decimal_point<Char>(loc) : static_cast<Char>('.'); 
1690   float_writer<Char> w(buffer.data(), static_cast<int>(buffer.size()), exp, 
1691                        fspecs, point); 
1692   return write_padded<align::right>(out, specs, w.size(), w); 
1693 } 
1694  
1695 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt, typename T, 
1696           FMT_ENABLE_IF(std::is_floating_point<T>::value)> 
1697 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, T value) { 
1698   if (const_check(!is_supported_floating_point(value))) return out; 
1699   auto fspecs = float_specs(); 
1700   if (std::signbit(value)) {  // value < 0 is false for NaN so use signbit. 
1701     fspecs.sign = sign::minus; 
1702     value = -value; 
1703   } 
1704  
1705   auto specs = basic_format_specs<Char>(); 
1706   if (!std::isfinite(value)) 
1707     return write_nonfinite(out, std::isinf(value), specs, fspecs); 
1708  
1709   memory_buffer buffer; 
1710   int precision = -1; 
1711   if (const_check(std::is_same<T, float>())) fspecs.binary32 = true; 
1712   fspecs.use_grisu = use_grisu<T>(); 
1713   int exp = format_float(promote_float(value), precision, fspecs, buffer); 
1714   fspecs.precision = precision; 
1715   float_writer<Char> w(buffer.data(), static_cast<int>(buffer.size()), exp, 
1716                        fspecs, static_cast<Char>('.')); 
1717   return base_iterator(out, w(reserve(out, w.size()))); 
1718 } 
1719  
1720 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt> 
1721 OutputIt write_char(OutputIt out, Char value, 
1722                     const basic_format_specs<Char>& specs) { 
1723   using iterator = remove_reference_t<decltype(reserve(out, 0))>; 
1724   return write_padded(out, specs, 1, [=](iterator it) { 
1725     *it++ = value; 
1726     return it; 
1727   }); 
1728 } 
1729  
1730 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt, typename UIntPtr> 
1731 OutputIt write_ptr(OutputIt out, UIntPtr value, 
1732                    const basic_format_specs<Char>* specs) { 
1733   int num_digits = count_digits<4>(value); 
1734   auto size = to_unsigned(num_digits) + size_t(2); 
1735   using iterator = remove_reference_t<decltype(reserve(out, 0))>; 
1736   auto write = [=](iterator it) { 
1737     *it++ = static_cast<Char>('0'); 
1738     *it++ = static_cast<Char>('x'); 
1739     return format_uint<4, Char>(it, value, num_digits); 
1740   }; 
1741   return specs ? write_padded<align::right>(out, *specs, size, write) 
1742                : base_iterator(out, write(reserve(out, size))); 
1743 } 
1744  
1745 template <typename T> struct is_integral : std::is_integral<T> {}; 
1746 template <> struct is_integral<int128_t> : std::true_type {}; 
1747 template <> struct is_integral<uint128_t> : std::true_type {}; 
1748  
1749 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt> 
1750 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, monostate) { 
1751   FMT_ASSERT(false, ""); 
1752   return out; 
1753 } 
1754  
1755 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt, 
1756           FMT_ENABLE_IF(!std::is_same<Char, char>::value)> 
1757 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, string_view value) { 
1758   auto it = reserve(out, value.size()); 
1759   it = copy_str<Char>(value.begin(), value.end(), it); 
1760   return base_iterator(out, it); 
1761 } 
1762  
1763 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt> 
1764 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, basic_string_view<Char> value) { 
1765   auto it = reserve(out, value.size()); 
1766   it = std::copy(value.begin(), value.end(), it); 
1767   return base_iterator(out, it); 
1768 } 
1769  
1770 template <typename Char> 
1771 buffer_appender<Char> write(buffer_appender<Char> out, 
1772                             basic_string_view<Char> value) { 
1773   get_container(out).append(value.begin(), value.end()); 
1774   return out; 
1775 } 
1776  
1777 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt, typename T, 
1778           FMT_ENABLE_IF(is_integral<T>::value && 
1779                         !std::is_same<T, bool>::value && 
1780                         !std::is_same<T, Char>::value)> 
1781 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, T value) { 
1782   auto abs_value = static_cast<uint32_or_64_or_128_t<T>>(value); 
1783   bool negative = is_negative(value); 
1784   // Don't do -abs_value since it trips unsigned-integer-overflow sanitizer. 
1785   if (negative) abs_value = ~abs_value + 1; 
1786   int num_digits = count_digits(abs_value); 
1787   auto size = (negative ? 1 : 0) + static_cast<size_t>(num_digits); 
1788   auto it = reserve(out, size); 
1789   if (auto ptr = to_pointer<Char>(it, size)) { 
1790     if (negative) *ptr++ = static_cast<Char>('-'); 
1791     format_decimal<Char>(ptr, abs_value, num_digits); 
1792     return out; 
1793   } 
1794   if (negative) *it++ = static_cast<Char>('-'); 
1795   it = format_decimal<Char>(it, abs_value, num_digits).end; 
1796   return base_iterator(out, it); 
1797 } 
1798  
1799 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt> 
1800 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, bool value) { 
1801   return write<Char>(out, string_view(value ? "true" : "false")); 
1802 } 
1803  
1804 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt> 
1805 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, Char value) { 
1806   auto it = reserve(out, 1); 
1807   *it++ = value; 
1808   return base_iterator(out, it); 
1809 } 
1810  
1811 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt> 
1812 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, const Char* value) { 
1813   if (!value) { 
1814     FMT_THROW(format_error("string pointer is null")); 
1815   } else { 
1816     auto length = std::char_traits<Char>::length(value); 
1817     out = write(out, basic_string_view<Char>(value, length)); 
1818   } 
1819   return out; 
1820 } 
1821  
1822 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt> 
1823 OutputIt write(OutputIt out, const void* value) { 
1824   return write_ptr<Char>(out, to_uintptr(value), nullptr); 
1825 } 
1826  
1827 template <typename Char, typename OutputIt, typename T> 
1828 auto write(OutputIt out, const T& value) -> typename std::enable_if< 
1829     mapped_type_constant<T, basic_format_context<OutputIt, Char>>::value == 
1830         type::custom_type, 
1831     OutputIt>::type { 
1832   basic_format_context<OutputIt, Char> ctx(out, {}, {}); 
1833   return formatter<T>().format(value, ctx); 
1834 }

1836 // An argument visitor that formats the argument and writes it via the output 
1837 // iterator. It's a class and not a generic lambda for compatibility with C++11. 
1838 template <typename OutputIt, typename Char> struct default_arg_formatter { 
1839   using context = basic_format_context<OutputIt, Char>; 
1840  
1841   OutputIt out; 
1842   basic_format_args<context> args; 
1843   locale_ref loc; 
1844  
1845   template <typename T> OutputIt operator()(T value) { 
1846     return write<Char>(out, value); 
1847   } 
1848  
1849   OutputIt operator()(typename basic_format_arg<context>::handle handle) { 
1850     basic_format_parse_context<Char> parse_ctx({}); 
1851     basic_format_context<OutputIt, Char> format_ctx(out, args, loc); 
1852     handle.format(parse_ctx, format_ctx); 
1853     return format_ctx.out(); 
1854   } 
1855 }; 
1856  
1857 template <typename OutputIt, typename Char, 
1858           typename ErrorHandler = error_handler> 
1859 class arg_formatter_base { 
1860  public: 
1861   using iterator = OutputIt; 
1862   using char_type = Char; 
1863   using format_specs = basic_format_specs<Char>; 
1864  
1865  private: 
1866   iterator out_; 
1867   locale_ref locale_; 
1868   format_specs* specs_; 
1869  
1870   // Attempts to reserve space for n extra characters in the output range. 
1871   // Returns a pointer to the reserved range or a reference to out_. 
1872   auto reserve(size_t n) -> decltype(detail::reserve(out_, n)) { 
1873     return detail::reserve(out_, n); 
1874   } 
1875  
1876   using reserve_iterator = remove_reference_t<decltype( 
1877       detail::reserve(std::declval<iterator&>(), 0))>; 
1878  
1879   template <typename T> void write_int(T value, const format_specs& spec) { 
1880     using uint_type = uint32_or_64_or_128_t<T>; 
1881     int_writer<iterator, Char, uint_type> w(out_, locale_, value, spec); 
1882     handle_int_type_spec(spec.type, w); 
1883     out_ = w.out; 
1884   } 
1885  
1886   void write(char value) { 
1887     auto&& it = reserve(1); 
1888     *it++ = value; 
1889   } 
1890  
1891   template <typename Ch, FMT_ENABLE_IF(std::is_same<Ch, Char>::value)> 
1892   void write(Ch value) { 
1893     out_ = detail::write<Char>(out_, value); 
1894   } 
1895  
1896   void write(string_view value) { 
1897     auto&& it = reserve(value.size()); 
1898     it = copy_str<Char>(value.begin(), value.end(), it); 
1899   } 
1900   void write(wstring_view value) { 
1901     static_assert(std::is_same<Char, wchar_t>::value, ""); 
1902     auto&& it = reserve(value.size()); 
1903     it = std::copy(value.begin(), value.end(), it); 
1904   } 
1905  
1906   template <typename Ch> 
1907   void write(const Ch* s, size_t size, const format_specs& specs) { 
1908     auto width = specs.width != 0 
1909                      ? count_code_points(basic_string_view<Ch>(s, size)) 
1910                      : 0; 
1911     out_ = write_padded(out_, specs, size, width, [=](reserve_iterator it) { 
1912       return copy_str<Char>(s, s + size, it); 
1913     }); 
1914   } 
1915  
1916   template <typename Ch> 
1917   void write(basic_string_view<Ch> s, const format_specs& specs = {}) { 
1918     out_ = detail::write(out_, s, specs); 
1919   } 
1920  
1921   void write_pointer(const void* p) { 
1922     out_ = write_ptr<char_type>(out_, to_uintptr(p), specs_); 
1923   } 
1924  
1925   struct char_spec_handler : ErrorHandler { 
1926     arg_formatter_base& formatter; 
1927     Char value; 
1928  
1929     char_spec_handler(arg_formatter_base& f, Char val) 
1930         : formatter(f), value(val) {} 
1931  
1932     void on_int() { 
1933       // char is only formatted as int if there are specs. 
1934       formatter.write_int(static_cast<int>(value), *formatter.specs_); 
1935     } 
1936     void on_char() { 
1937       if (formatter.specs_) 
1938         formatter.out_ = write_char(formatter.out_, value, *formatter.specs_); 
1939       else 
1940         formatter.write(value); 
1941     } 
1942   }; 
1943  
1944   struct cstring_spec_handler : error_handler { 
1945     arg_formatter_base& formatter; 
1946     const Char* value; 
1947  
1948     cstring_spec_handler(arg_formatter_base& f, const Char* val) 
1949         : formatter(f), value(val) {} 
1950  
1951     void on_string() { formatter.write(value); } 
1952     void on_pointer() { formatter.write_pointer(value); } 
1953   }; 
1954  
1955  protected: 
1956   iterator out() { return out_; } 
1957   format_specs* specs() { return specs_; } 
1958  
1959   void write(bool value) { 
1960     if (specs_) 
1961       write(string_view(value ? "true" : "false"), *specs_); 
1962     else 
1963       out_ = detail::write<Char>(out_, value); 
1964   } 
1965  
1966   void write(const Char* value) { 
1967     if (!value) { 
1968       FMT_THROW(format_error("string pointer is null")); 
1969     } else { 
1970       auto length = std::char_traits<char_type>::length(value); 
1971       basic_string_view<char_type> sv(value, length); 
1972       specs_ ? write(sv, *specs_) : write(sv); 
1973     } 
1974   } 
1975  
1976  public: 
1977   arg_formatter_base(OutputIt out, format_specs* s, locale_ref loc) 
1978       : out_(out), locale_(loc), specs_(s) {} 
1979  
1980   iterator operator()(monostate) { 
1981     FMT_ASSERT(false, "invalid argument type"); 
1982     return out_; 
1983   } 
1984  
1985   template <typename T, FMT_ENABLE_IF(is_integral<T>::value)> 
1986   FMT_INLINE iterator operator()(T value) { 
1987     if (specs_) 
1988       write_int(value, *specs_); 
1989     else 
1990       out_ = detail::write<Char>(out_, value); 
1991     return out_; 
1992   } 
1993  
1994   iterator operator()(Char value) { 
1995     handle_char_specs(specs_, 
1996                       char_spec_handler(*this, static_cast<Char>(value))); 
1997     return out_; 
1998   } 
1999  
2000   iterator operator()(bool value) { 
2001     if (specs_ && specs_->type) return (*this)(value ? 1 : 0); 
2002     write(value != 0); 
2003     return out_; 
2004   } 
2005  
2006   template <typename T, FMT_ENABLE_IF(std::is_floating_point<T>::value)> 
2007   iterator operator()(T value) { 
2008     auto specs = specs_ ? *specs_ : format_specs(); 
2009     if (const_check(is_supported_floating_point(value))) 
2010       out_ = detail::write(out_, value, specs, locale_); 
2011     else 
2012       FMT_ASSERT(false, "unsupported float argument type"); 
2013     return out_; 
2014   } 
2015  
2016   iterator operator()(const Char* value) { 
2017     if (!specs_) return write(value), out_; 
2018     handle_cstring_type_spec(specs_->type, cstring_spec_handler(*this, value)); 
2019     return out_; 
2020   } 
2021  
2022   iterator operator()(basic_string_view<Char> value) { 
2023     if (specs_) { 
2024       check_string_type_spec(specs_->type, error_handler()); 
2025       write(value, *specs_); 
2026     } else { 
2027       write(value); 
2028     } 
2029     return out_; 
2030   } 
2031  
2032   iterator operator()(const void* value) { 
2033     if (specs_) check_pointer_type_spec(specs_->type, error_handler()); 
2034     write_pointer(value); 
2035     return out_; 
2036   } 
2037 };

2039 template <typename Char> FMT_CONSTEXPR bool is_name_start(Char c) { 
2040   return ('a' <= c && c <= 'z') || ('A' <= c && c <= 'Z') || '_' == c; 
2041 } 
2042  
2043 // Parses the range [begin, end) as an unsigned integer. This function assumes 
2044 // that the range is non-empty and the first character is a digit. 
2045 template <typename Char, typename ErrorHandler> 
2046 FMT_CONSTEXPR int parse_nonnegative_int(const Char*& begin, const Char* end, 
2047                                         ErrorHandler&& eh) { 
2048   FMT_ASSERT(begin != end && '0' <= *begin && *begin <= '9', ""); 
2049   unsigned value = 0; 
2050   // Convert to unsigned to prevent a warning. 
2051   constexpr unsigned max_int = max_value<int>(); 
2052   unsigned big = max_int / 10; 
2053   do { 
2054     // Check for overflow. 
2055     if (value > big) { 
2056       value = max_int + 1; 
2057       break; 
2058     } 
2059     value = value * 10 + unsigned(*begin - '0'); 
2060     ++begin; 
2061   } while (begin != end && '0' <= *begin && *begin <= '9'); 
2062   if (value > max_int) eh.on_error("number is too big"); 
2063   return static_cast<int>(value); 
2064 }

2071   Context& ctx_; 
2072  
2073  public: 
2074   explicit custom_formatter(basic_format_parse_context<char_type>& parse_ctx, 
2075                             Context& ctx) 
2076       : parse_ctx_(parse_ctx), ctx_(ctx) {} 
2077  
2078   bool operator()(typename basic_format_arg<Context>::handle h) const { 
2079     h.format(parse_ctx_, ctx_); 
2080     return true; 
2081   } 
2082  
2083   template <typename T> bool operator()(T) const { return false; } 
2084 };

2100   }

2103   FMT_CONSTEXPR unsigned long long operator()(T) { 
2104     handler_.on_error("width is not integer"); 
2105     return 0; 
2106   }

2108  private: 
2109   ErrorHandler& handler_; 
2110 };

2132 // A format specifier handler that sets fields in basic_format_specs. 
2133 template <typename Char> class specs_setter { 
2134  public: 
2135   explicit FMT_CONSTEXPR specs_setter(basic_format_specs<Char>& specs) 
2136       : specs_(specs) {} 
2137  
2138   FMT_CONSTEXPR specs_setter(const specs_setter& other) 
2139       : specs_(other.specs_) {} 
2140  
2141   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_align(align_t align) { specs_.align = align; } 
2142   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_fill(basic_string_view<Char> fill) { 
2143     specs_.fill = fill; 
2144   } 
2145   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_plus() { specs_.sign = sign::plus; } 
2146   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_minus() { specs_.sign = sign::minus; } 
2147   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_space() { specs_.sign = sign::space; } 
2148   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_hash() { specs_.alt = true; } 
2149  
2150   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_zero() { 
2151     specs_.align = align::numeric; 
2152     specs_.fill[0] = Char('0'); 
2153   } 
2154  
2155   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_width(int width) { specs_.width = width; } 
2156   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_precision(int precision) { 
2157     specs_.precision = precision; 
2158   } 
2159   FMT_CONSTEXPR void end_precision() {} 
2160  
2161   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_type(Char type) { 
2162     specs_.type = static_cast<char>(type); 
2163   } 
2164  
2165  protected: 
2166   basic_format_specs<Char>& specs_; 
2167 };

2169 template <typename ErrorHandler> class numeric_specs_checker { 
2170  public: 
2171   FMT_CONSTEXPR numeric_specs_checker(ErrorHandler& eh, detail::type arg_type) 
2172       : error_handler_(eh), arg_type_(arg_type) {} 
2173  
2174   FMT_CONSTEXPR void require_numeric_argument() { 
2175     if (!is_arithmetic_type(arg_type_)) 
2176       error_handler_.on_error("format specifier requires numeric argument"); 
2177   } 
2178  
2179   FMT_CONSTEXPR void check_sign() { 
2180     require_numeric_argument(); 
2181     if (is_integral_type(arg_type_) && arg_type_ != type::int_type && 
2182         arg_type_ != type::long_long_type && arg_type_ != type::char_type) { 
2183       error_handler_.on_error("format specifier requires signed argument"); 
2184     } 
2185   } 
2186  
2187   FMT_CONSTEXPR void check_precision() { 
2188     if (is_integral_type(arg_type_) || arg_type_ == type::pointer_type) 
2189       error_handler_.on_error("precision not allowed for this argument type"); 
2190   } 
2191  
2192  private: 
2193   ErrorHandler& error_handler_; 
2194   detail::type arg_type_; 
2195 };

2197 // A format specifier handler that checks if specifiers are consistent with the 
2198 // argument type. 
2199 template <typename Handler> class specs_checker : public Handler { 
2200  private: 
2201   numeric_specs_checker<Handler> checker_; 
2202  
2203   // Suppress an MSVC warning about using this in initializer list. 
2204   FMT_CONSTEXPR Handler& error_handler() { return *this; } 
2205  
2206  public: 
2207   FMT_CONSTEXPR specs_checker(const Handler& handler, detail::type arg_type) 
2208       : Handler(handler), checker_(error_handler(), arg_type) {} 
2209  
2210   FMT_CONSTEXPR specs_checker(const specs_checker& other) 
2211       : Handler(other), checker_(error_handler(), other.arg_type_) {} 
2212  
2213   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_align(align_t align) { 
2214     if (align == align::numeric) checker_.require_numeric_argument(); 
2215     Handler::on_align(align); 
2216   } 
2217  
2218   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_plus() { 
2219     checker_.check_sign(); 
2220     Handler::on_plus(); 
2221   } 
2222  
2223   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_minus() { 
2224     checker_.check_sign(); 
2225     Handler::on_minus(); 
2226   } 
2227  
2228   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_space() { 
2229     checker_.check_sign(); 
2230     Handler::on_space(); 
2231   } 
2232  
2233   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_hash() { 
2234     checker_.require_numeric_argument(); 
2235     Handler::on_hash(); 
2236   } 
2237  
2238   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_zero() { 
2239     checker_.require_numeric_argument(); 
2240     Handler::on_zero(); 
2241   } 
2242  
2243   FMT_CONSTEXPR void end_precision() { checker_.check_precision(); } 
2244 };

2246 template <template <typename> class Handler, typename FormatArg, 
2247           typename ErrorHandler> 
2248 FMT_CONSTEXPR int get_dynamic_spec(FormatArg arg, ErrorHandler eh) { 
2249   unsigned long long value = visit_format_arg(Handler<ErrorHandler>(eh), arg); 
2250   if (value > to_unsigned(max_value<int>())) eh.on_error("number is too big"); 
2251   return static_cast<int>(value); 
2252 }

2263 // The standard format specifier handler with checking. 
2264 template <typename ParseContext, typename Context> 
2265 class specs_handler : public specs_setter<typename Context::char_type> { 
2266  public: 
2267   using char_type = typename Context::char_type; 
2268  
2269   FMT_CONSTEXPR specs_handler(basic_format_specs<char_type>& specs, 
2270                               ParseContext& parse_ctx, Context& ctx) 
2271       : specs_setter<char_type>(specs), 
2272         parse_context_(parse_ctx), 
2273         context_(ctx) {} 
2274  
2275   template <typename Id> FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_dynamic_width(Id arg_id) { 
2276     this->specs_.width = get_dynamic_spec<width_checker>( 
2277         get_arg(arg_id), context_.error_handler()); 
2278   } 
2279  
2280   template <typename Id> FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_dynamic_precision(Id arg_id) { 
2281     this->specs_.precision = get_dynamic_spec<precision_checker>( 
2282         get_arg(arg_id), context_.error_handler()); 
2283   } 
2284  
2285   void on_error(const char* message) { context_.on_error(message); } 
2286  
2287  private: 
2288   // This is only needed for compatibility with gcc 4.4. 
2289   using format_arg = typename Context::format_arg; 
2290  
2291   FMT_CONSTEXPR format_arg get_arg(auto_id) { 
2292     return detail::get_arg(context_, parse_context_.next_arg_id()); 
2293   } 
2294  
2295   FMT_CONSTEXPR format_arg get_arg(int arg_id) { 
2296     parse_context_.check_arg_id(arg_id); 
2297     return detail::get_arg(context_, arg_id); 
2298   } 
2299  
2300   FMT_CONSTEXPR format_arg get_arg(basic_string_view<char_type> arg_id) { 
2301     parse_context_.check_arg_id(arg_id); 
2302     return detail::get_arg(context_, arg_id); 
2303   } 
2304  
2305   ParseContext& parse_context_; 
2306   Context& context_; 
2307 }; 
2308  
2309 enum class arg_id_kind { none, index, name }; 
2310  
2311 // An argument reference. 
2312 template <typename Char> struct arg_ref { 
2313   FMT_CONSTEXPR arg_ref() : kind(arg_id_kind::none), val() {} 
2314  
2315   FMT_CONSTEXPR explicit arg_ref(int index) 
2316       : kind(arg_id_kind::index), val(index) {} 
2317   FMT_CONSTEXPR explicit arg_ref(basic_string_view<Char> name) 
2318       : kind(arg_id_kind::name), val(name) {} 
2319  
2320   FMT_CONSTEXPR arg_ref& operator=(int idx) { 
2321     kind = arg_id_kind::index; 
2322     val.index = idx; 
2323     return *this; 
2324   } 
2325  
2326   arg_id_kind kind; 
2327   union value { 
2328     FMT_CONSTEXPR value(int id = 0) : index{id} {} 
2329     FMT_CONSTEXPR value(basic_string_view<Char> n) : name(n) {} 
2330  
2331     int index; 
2332     basic_string_view<Char> name; 
2333   } val; 
2334 }; 
2335  
2336 // Format specifiers with width and precision resolved at formatting rather 
2337 // than parsing time to allow re-using the same parsed specifiers with 
2338 // different sets of arguments (precompilation of format strings). 
2339 template <typename Char> 
2340 struct dynamic_format_specs : basic_format_specs<Char> { 
2341   arg_ref<Char> width_ref; 
2342   arg_ref<Char> precision_ref; 
2343 }; 
2344  
2345 // Format spec handler that saves references to arguments representing dynamic 
2346 // width and precision to be resolved at formatting time. 
2347 template <typename ParseContext> 
2348 class dynamic_specs_handler 
2349     : public specs_setter<typename ParseContext::char_type> { 
2350  public: 
2351   using char_type = typename ParseContext::char_type; 
2352  
2353   FMT_CONSTEXPR dynamic_specs_handler(dynamic_format_specs<char_type>& specs, 
2354                                       ParseContext& ctx) 
2355       : specs_setter<char_type>(specs), specs_(specs), context_(ctx) {} 
2356  
2357   FMT_CONSTEXPR dynamic_specs_handler(const dynamic_specs_handler& other) 
2358       : specs_setter<char_type>(other), 
2359         specs_(other.specs_), 
2360         context_(other.context_) {} 
2361  
2362   template <typename Id> FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_dynamic_width(Id arg_id) { 
2363     specs_.width_ref = make_arg_ref(arg_id); 
2364   } 
2365  
2366   template <typename Id> FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_dynamic_precision(Id arg_id) { 
2367     specs_.precision_ref = make_arg_ref(arg_id); 
2368   } 
2369  
2370   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_error(const char* message) { 
2371     context_.on_error(message); 
2372   } 
2373  
2374  private: 
2375   using arg_ref_type = arg_ref<char_type>; 
2376  
2377   FMT_CONSTEXPR arg_ref_type make_arg_ref(int arg_id) { 
2378     context_.check_arg_id(arg_id); 
2379     return arg_ref_type(arg_id); 
2380   } 
2381  
2382   FMT_CONSTEXPR arg_ref_type make_arg_ref(auto_id) { 
2383     return arg_ref_type(context_.next_arg_id()); 
2384   } 
2385  
2386   FMT_CONSTEXPR arg_ref_type make_arg_ref(basic_string_view<char_type> arg_id) { 
2387     context_.check_arg_id(arg_id); 
2388     basic_string_view<char_type> format_str( 
2389         context_.begin(), to_unsigned(context_.end() - context_.begin())); 
2390     return arg_ref_type(arg_id); 
2391   } 
2392  
2393   dynamic_format_specs<char_type>& specs_; 
2394   ParseContext& context_; 
2395 }; 
2396  
2397 template <typename Char, typename IDHandler> 
2398 FMT_CONSTEXPR const Char* parse_arg_id(const Char* begin, const Char* end, 
2399                                        IDHandler&& handler) { 
2400   FMT_ASSERT(begin != end, ""); 
2401   Char c = *begin; 
2402   if (c == '}' || c == ':') { 
2403     handler(); 
2404     return begin; 
2405   } 
2406   if (c >= '0' && c <= '9') { 
2407     int index = 0; 
2408     if (c != '0') 
2409       index = parse_nonnegative_int(begin, end, handler); 
2410     else 
2411       ++begin; 
2412     if (begin == end || (*begin != '}' && *begin != ':')) 
2413       handler.on_error("invalid format string"); 
2414     else 
2415       handler(index); 
2416     return begin; 
2417   } 
2418   if (!is_name_start(c)) { 
2419     handler.on_error("invalid format string"); 
2420     return begin; 
2421   } 
2422   auto it = begin; 
2423   do { 
2424     ++it; 
2425   } while (it != end && (is_name_start(c = *it) || ('0' <= c && c <= '9'))); 
2426   handler(basic_string_view<Char>(begin, to_unsigned(it - begin))); 
2427   return it; 
2428 } 
2429  
2430 // Adapts SpecHandler to IDHandler API for dynamic width. 
2431 template <typename SpecHandler, typename Char> struct width_adapter { 
2432   explicit FMT_CONSTEXPR width_adapter(SpecHandler& h) : handler(h) {} 
2433  
2434   FMT_CONSTEXPR void operator()() { handler.on_dynamic_width(auto_id()); } 
2435   FMT_CONSTEXPR void operator()(int id) { handler.on_dynamic_width(id); } 
2436   FMT_CONSTEXPR void operator()(basic_string_view<Char> id) { 
2437     handler.on_dynamic_width(id); 
2438   } 
2439  
2440   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_error(const char* message) { 
2441     handler.on_error(message); 
2442   } 
2443  
2444   SpecHandler& handler; 
2445 }; 
2446  
2447 // Adapts SpecHandler to IDHandler API for dynamic precision. 
2448 template <typename SpecHandler, typename Char> struct precision_adapter { 
2449   explicit FMT_CONSTEXPR precision_adapter(SpecHandler& h) : handler(h) {} 
2450  
2451   FMT_CONSTEXPR void operator()() { handler.on_dynamic_precision(auto_id()); } 
2452   FMT_CONSTEXPR void operator()(int id) { handler.on_dynamic_precision(id); } 
2453   FMT_CONSTEXPR void operator()(basic_string_view<Char> id) { 
2454     handler.on_dynamic_precision(id); 
2455   } 
2456  
2457   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_error(const char* message) { 
2458     handler.on_error(message); 
2459   } 
2460  
2461   SpecHandler& handler; 
2462 }; 
2463  
2464 template <typename Char> 
2465 FMT_CONSTEXPR const Char* next_code_point(const Char* begin, const Char* end) { 
2466   if (const_check(sizeof(Char) != 1) || (*begin & 0x80) == 0) return begin + 1; 
2467   do { 
2468     ++begin; 
2469   } while (begin != end && (*begin & 0xc0) == 0x80); 
2470   return begin; 
2471 } 
2472  
2473 // Parses fill and alignment. 
2474 template <typename Char, typename Handler> 
2475 FMT_CONSTEXPR const Char* parse_align(const Char* begin, const Char* end, 
2476                                       Handler&& handler) { 
2477   FMT_ASSERT(begin != end, ""); 
2478   auto align = align::none; 
2479   auto p = next_code_point(begin, end); 
2480   if (p == end) p = begin; 
2481   for (;;) { 
2482     switch (static_cast<char>(*p)) { 
2483     case '<': 
2484       align = align::left; 
2485       break; 
2486     case '>': 
2487       align = align::right; 
2488       break; 
2489 #if FMT_DEPRECATED_NUMERIC_ALIGN 
2490     case '=': 
2491       align = align::numeric; 
2492       break; 
2493 #endif 
2494     case '^': 
2495       align = align::center; 
2496       break; 
2497     } 
2498     if (align != align::none) { 
2499       if (p != begin) { 
2500         auto c = *begin; 
2501         if (c == '{') 
2502           return handler.on_error("invalid fill character '{'"), begin; 
2503         handler.on_fill(basic_string_view<Char>(begin, to_unsigned(p - begin))); 
2504         begin = p + 1; 
2505       } else 
2506         ++begin; 
2507       handler.on_align(align); 
2508       break; 
2509     } else if (p == begin) { 
2510       break; 
2511     } 
2512     p = begin; 
2513   } 
2514   return begin; 
2515 } 
2516  
2517 template <typename Char, typename Handler> 
2518 FMT_CONSTEXPR const Char* parse_width(const Char* begin, const Char* end, 
2519                                       Handler&& handler) { 
2520   FMT_ASSERT(begin != end, ""); 
2521   if ('0' <= *begin && *begin <= '9') { 
2522     handler.on_width(parse_nonnegative_int(begin, end, handler)); 
2523   } else if (*begin == '{') { 
2524     ++begin; 
2525     if (begin != end) 
2526       begin = parse_arg_id(begin, end, width_adapter<Handler, Char>(handler)); 
2527     if (begin == end || *begin != '}') 
2528       return handler.on_error("invalid format string"), begin; 
2529     ++begin; 
2530   } 
2531   return begin; 
2532 } 
2533  
2534 template <typename Char, typename Handler> 
2535 FMT_CONSTEXPR const Char* parse_precision(const Char* begin, const Char* end, 
2536                                           Handler&& handler) { 
2537   ++begin; 
2538   auto c = begin != end ? *begin : Char(); 
2539   if ('0' <= c && c <= '9') { 
2540     handler.on_precision(parse_nonnegative_int(begin, end, handler)); 
2541   } else if (c == '{') { 
2542     ++begin; 
2543     if (begin != end) { 
2544       begin = 
2545           parse_arg_id(begin, end, precision_adapter<Handler, Char>(handler)); 
2546     } 
2547     if (begin == end || *begin++ != '}') 
2548       return handler.on_error("invalid format string"), begin; 
2549   } else { 
2550     return handler.on_error("missing precision specifier"), begin; 
2551   } 
2552   handler.end_precision(); 
2553   return begin; 
2554 } 
2555  
2556 // Parses standard format specifiers and sends notifications about parsed 
2557 // components to handler. 
2558 template <typename Char, typename SpecHandler> 
2559 FMT_CONSTEXPR const Char* parse_format_specs(const Char* begin, const Char* end, 
2560                                              SpecHandler&& handler) { 
2561   if (begin == end || *begin == '}') return begin; 
2562  
2563   begin = parse_align(begin, end, handler); 
2564   if (begin == end) return begin; 
2565  
2566   // Parse sign. 
2567   switch (static_cast<char>(*begin)) { 
2568   case '+': 
2569     handler.on_plus(); 
2570     ++begin; 
2571     break; 
2572   case '-': 
2573     handler.on_minus(); 
2574     ++begin; 
2575     break; 
2576   case ' ': 
2577     handler.on_space(); 
2578     ++begin; 
2579     break; 
2580   } 
2581   if (begin == end) return begin; 
2582  
2583   if (*begin == '#') { 
2584     handler.on_hash(); 
2585     if (++begin == end) return begin; 
2586   } 
2587  
2588   // Parse zero flag. 
2589   if (*begin == '0') { 
2590     handler.on_zero(); 
2591     if (++begin == end) return begin; 
2592   } 
2593  
2594   begin = parse_width(begin, end, handler); 
2595   if (begin == end) return begin; 
2596  
2597   // Parse precision. 
2598   if (*begin == '.') { 
2599     begin = parse_precision(begin, end, handler); 
2600   } 
2601  
2602   // Parse type. 
2603   if (begin != end && *begin != '}') handler.on_type(*begin++); 
2604   return begin; 
2605 } 
2606  
2607 // Return the result via the out param to workaround gcc bug 77539. 
2608 template <bool IS_CONSTEXPR, typename T, typename Ptr = const T*> 
2609 FMT_CONSTEXPR bool find(Ptr first, Ptr last, T value, Ptr& out) { 
2610   for (out = first; out != last; ++out) { 
2611     if (*out == value) return true; 
2612   } 
2613   return false; 
2614 } 
2615  
2616 template <> 
2617 inline bool find<false, char>(const char* first, const char* last, char value, 
2618                               const char*& out) { 
2619   out = static_cast<const char*>( 
2620       std::memchr(first, value, detail::to_unsigned(last - first))); 
2621   return out != nullptr; 
2622 } 
2623  
2624 template <typename Handler, typename Char> struct id_adapter { 
2625   Handler& handler; 
2626   int arg_id; 
2627  
2628   FMT_CONSTEXPR void operator()() { arg_id = handler.on_arg_id(); } 
2629   FMT_CONSTEXPR void operator()(int id) { arg_id = handler.on_arg_id(id); } 
2630   FMT_CONSTEXPR void operator()(basic_string_view<Char> id) { 
2631     arg_id = handler.on_arg_id(id); 
2632   } 
2633   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_error(const char* message) { 
2634     handler.on_error(message); 
2635   } 
2636 }; 
2637  
2638 template <typename Char, typename Handler> 
2639 FMT_CONSTEXPR const Char* parse_replacement_field(const Char* begin, 
2640                                                   const Char* end, 
2641                                                   Handler&& handler) { 
2642   ++begin; 
2643   if (begin == end) return handler.on_error("invalid format string"), end; 
2644   if (static_cast<char>(*begin) == '}') { 
2645     handler.on_replacement_field(handler.on_arg_id(), begin); 
2646   } else if (*begin == '{') { 
2647     handler.on_text(begin, begin + 1); 
2648   } else { 
2649     auto adapter = id_adapter<Handler, Char>{handler, 0}; 
2650     begin = parse_arg_id(begin, end, adapter); 
2651     Char c = begin != end ? *begin : Char(); 
2652     if (c == '}') { 
2653       handler.on_replacement_field(adapter.arg_id, begin); 
2654     } else if (c == ':') { 
2655       begin = handler.on_format_specs(adapter.arg_id, begin + 1, end); 
2656       if (begin == end || *begin != '}') 
2657         return handler.on_error("unknown format specifier"), end; 
2658     } else { 
2659       return handler.on_error("missing '}' in format string"), end; 
2660     } 
2661   } 
2662   return begin + 1; 
2663 } 
2664  
2665 template <bool IS_CONSTEXPR, typename Char, typename Handler> 
2666 FMT_CONSTEXPR_DECL FMT_INLINE void parse_format_string( 
2667     basic_string_view<Char> format_str, Handler&& handler) { 
2668   auto begin = format_str.data(); 
2669   auto end = begin + format_str.size(); 
2670   if (end - begin < 32) { 
2671     // Use a simple loop instead of memchr for small strings. 
2672     const Char* p = begin; 
2673     while (p != end) { 
2674       auto c = *p++; 
2675       if (c == '{') { 
2676         handler.on_text(begin, p - 1); 
2677         begin = p = parse_replacement_field(p - 1, end, handler); 
2678       } else if (c == '}') { 
2679         if (p == end || *p != '}') 
2680           return handler.on_error("unmatched '}' in format string"); 
2681         handler.on_text(begin, p); 
2682         begin = ++p; 
2683       } 
2684     } 
2685     handler.on_text(begin, end); 
2686     return; 
2687   } 
2688   struct writer { 
2689     FMT_CONSTEXPR void operator()(const Char* begin, const Char* end) { 
2690       if (begin == end) return; 
2691       for (;;) { 
2692         const Char* p = nullptr; 
2693         if (!find<IS_CONSTEXPR>(begin, end, '}', p)) 
2694           return handler_.on_text(begin, end); 
2695         ++p; 
2696         if (p == end || *p != '}') 
2697           return handler_.on_error("unmatched '}' in format string"); 
2698         handler_.on_text(begin, p); 
2699         begin = p + 1; 
2700       } 
2701     } 
2702     Handler& handler_; 
2703   } write{handler}; 
2704   while (begin != end) { 
2705     // Doing two passes with memchr (one for '{' and another for '}') is up to 
2706     // 2.5x faster than the naive one-pass implementation on big format strings. 
2707     const Char* p = begin; 
2708     if (*begin != '{' && !find<IS_CONSTEXPR>(begin + 1, end, '{', p)) 
2709       return write(begin, end); 
2710     write(begin, p); 
2711     begin = parse_replacement_field(p, end, handler); 
2712   } 
2713 } 
2714  
2715 template <typename T, typename ParseContext> 
2716 FMT_CONSTEXPR const typename ParseContext::char_type* parse_format_specs( 
2717     ParseContext& ctx) { 
2718   using char_type = typename ParseContext::char_type; 
2719   using context = buffer_context<char_type>; 
2720   using mapped_type = 
2721       conditional_t<detail::mapped_type_constant<T, context>::value != 
2722                         type::custom_type, 
2723                     decltype(arg_mapper<context>().map(std::declval<T>())), T>; 
2724   auto f = conditional_t<has_formatter<mapped_type, context>::value, 
2725                          formatter<mapped_type, char_type>, 
2726                          detail::fallback_formatter<T, char_type>>(); 
2727   return f.parse(ctx); 
2728 } 
2729  
2730 template <typename ArgFormatter, typename Char, typename Context> 
2731 struct format_handler : detail::error_handler { 
2732   basic_format_parse_context<Char> parse_context; 
2733   Context context; 
2734  
2735   format_handler(typename ArgFormatter::iterator out, 
2736                  basic_string_view<Char> str, 
2737                  basic_format_args<Context> format_args, detail::locale_ref loc) 
2738       : parse_context(str), context(out, format_args, loc) {} 
2739  
2740   void on_text(const Char* begin, const Char* end) { 
2741     auto size = to_unsigned(end - begin); 
2742     auto out = context.out(); 
2743     auto&& it = reserve(out, size); 
2744     it = std::copy_n(begin, size, it); 
2745     context.advance_to(out); 
2746   } 
2747  
2748   int on_arg_id() { return parse_context.next_arg_id(); } 
2749   int on_arg_id(int id) { return parse_context.check_arg_id(id), id; } 
2750   int on_arg_id(basic_string_view<Char> id) { 
2751     int arg_id = context.arg_id(id); 
2752     if (arg_id < 0) on_error("argument not found"); 
2753     return arg_id; 
2754   } 
2755  
2756   FMT_INLINE void on_replacement_field(int id, const Char*) { 
2757     auto arg = get_arg(context, id); 
2758     context.advance_to(visit_format_arg( 
2759         default_arg_formatter<typename ArgFormatter::iterator, Char>{ 
2760             context.out(), context.args(), context.locale()}, 
2761         arg)); 
2762   } 
2763  
2764   const Char* on_format_specs(int id, const Char* begin, const Char* end) { 
2765     advance_to(parse_context, begin); 
2766     auto arg = get_arg(context, id); 
2767     custom_formatter<Context> f(parse_context, context); 
2768     if (visit_format_arg(f, arg)) return parse_context.begin(); 
2769     basic_format_specs<Char> specs; 
2770     using parse_context_t = basic_format_parse_context<Char>; 
2771     specs_checker<specs_handler<parse_context_t, Context>> handler( 
2772         specs_handler<parse_context_t, Context>(specs, parse_context, context), 
2773         arg.type()); 
2774     begin = parse_format_specs(begin, end, handler); 
2775     if (begin == end || *begin != '}') on_error("missing '}' in format string"); 
2776     advance_to(parse_context, begin); 
2777     context.advance_to( 
2778         visit_format_arg(ArgFormatter(context, &parse_context, &specs), arg)); 
2779     return begin; 
2780   } 
2781 }; 
2782  
2783 // A parse context with extra argument id checks. It is only used at compile 
2784 // time because adding checks at runtime would introduce substantial overhead 
2785 // and would be redundant since argument ids are checked when arguments are 
2786 // retrieved anyway. 
2787 template <typename Char, typename ErrorHandler = error_handler> 
2788 class compile_parse_context 
2789     : public basic_format_parse_context<Char, ErrorHandler> { 
2790  private: 
2791   int num_args_; 
2792   using base = basic_format_parse_context<Char, ErrorHandler>; 
2793  
2794  public: 
2795   explicit FMT_CONSTEXPR compile_parse_context( 
2796       basic_string_view<Char> format_str, int num_args = max_value<int>(), 
2797       ErrorHandler eh = {}) 
2798       : base(format_str, eh), num_args_(num_args) {} 
2799  
2800   FMT_CONSTEXPR int next_arg_id() { 
2801     int id = base::next_arg_id(); 
2802     if (id >= num_args_) this->on_error("argument not found"); 
2803     return id; 
2804   } 
2805  
2806   FMT_CONSTEXPR void check_arg_id(int id) { 
2807     base::check_arg_id(id); 
2808     if (id >= num_args_) this->on_error("argument not found"); 
2809   } 
2810   using base::check_arg_id; 
2811 }; 
2812  
2813 template <typename Char, typename ErrorHandler, typename... Args> 
2814 class format_string_checker { 
2815  public: 
2816   explicit FMT_CONSTEXPR format_string_checker( 
2817       basic_string_view<Char> format_str, ErrorHandler eh) 
2818       : context_(format_str, num_args, eh), 
2819         parse_funcs_{&parse_format_specs<Args, parse_context_type>...} {} 
2820  
2821   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_text(const Char*, const Char*) {} 
2822  
2823   FMT_CONSTEXPR int on_arg_id() { return context_.next_arg_id(); } 
2824   FMT_CONSTEXPR int on_arg_id(int id) { return context_.check_arg_id(id), id; } 
2825   FMT_CONSTEXPR int on_arg_id(basic_string_view<Char>) { 
2826     on_error("compile-time checks don't support named arguments"); 
2827     return 0; 
2828   } 
2829  
2830   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_replacement_field(int, const Char*) {} 
2831  
2832   FMT_CONSTEXPR const Char* on_format_specs(int id, const Char* begin, 
2833                                             const Char*) { 
2834     advance_to(context_, begin); 
2835     return id < num_args ? parse_funcs_[id](context_) : begin; 
2836   } 
2837  
2838   FMT_CONSTEXPR void on_error(const char* message) { 
2839     context_.on_error(message); 
2840   } 
2841  
2842  private: 
2843   using parse_context_type = compile_parse_context<Char, ErrorHandler>; 
2844   enum { num_args = sizeof...(Args) }; 
2845  
2846   // Format specifier parsing function. 
2847   using parse_func = const Char* (*)(parse_context_type&); 
2848  
2849   parse_context_type context_; 
2850   parse_func parse_funcs_[num_args > 0 ? num_args : 1]; 
2851 }; 
2852  
2853 // Converts string literals to basic_string_view. 
2854 template <typename Char, size_t N> 
2855 FMT_CONSTEXPR basic_string_view<Char> compile_string_to_view( 
2856     const Char (&s)[N]) { 
2857   // Remove trailing null character if needed. Won't be present if this is used 
2858   // with raw character array (i.e. not defined as a string). 
2859   return {s, 
2860           N - ((std::char_traits<Char>::to_int_type(s[N - 1]) == 0) ? 1 : 0)}; 
2861 } 
2862  
2863 // Converts string_view to basic_string_view. 
2864 template <typename Char> 
2865 FMT_CONSTEXPR basic_string_view<Char> compile_string_to_view( 
2866     const std_string_view<Char>& s) { 
2867   return {s.data(), s.size()}; 
2868 } 
2869  
2870 #define FMT_STRING_IMPL(s, base)                                  \ 
2871   [] {                                                            \ 
2872     /* Use a macro-like name to avoid shadowing warnings. */      \ 
2873     struct FMT_COMPILE_STRING : base {                            \ 
2874       using char_type = fmt::remove_cvref_t<decltype(s[0])>;      \ 
2875       FMT_MAYBE_UNUSED FMT_CONSTEXPR                              \ 
2876       operator fmt::basic_string_view<char_type>() const {        \ 
2877         return fmt::detail::compile_string_to_view<char_type>(s); \ 
2878       }                                                           \ 
2879     };                                                            \ 
2880     return FMT_COMPILE_STRING();                                  \ 
2881   }() 
2882  
2883 /** 
2884   \rst 
2885   Constructs a compile-time format string from a string literal *s*. 
2886  
2887   **Example**:: 
2888  
2889     // A compile-time error because 'd' is an invalid specifier for strings. 
2890     std::string s = fmt::format(FMT_STRING("{:d}"), "foo"); 
2891   \endrst 
2892  */ 
2893 #define FMT_STRING(s) FMT_STRING_IMPL(s, fmt::compile_string) 
2894  
2895 template <typename... Args, typename S, 
2896           enable_if_t<(is_compile_string<S>::value), int>> 
2897 void check_format_string(S format_str) { 
2898   FMT_CONSTEXPR_DECL auto s = to_string_view(format_str); 
2899   using checker = format_string_checker<typename S::char_type, error_handler, 
2900                                         remove_cvref_t<Args>...>; 
2901   FMT_CONSTEXPR_DECL bool invalid_format = 
2902       (parse_format_string<true>(s, checker(s, {})), true); 
2903   (void)invalid_format; 
2904 } 
2905  
2906 template <template <typename> class Handler, typename Context> 
2907 void handle_dynamic_spec(int& value, arg_ref<typename Context::char_type> ref, 
2908                          Context& ctx) { 
2909   switch (ref.kind) { 
2910   case arg_id_kind::none: 
2911     break; 
2912   case arg_id_kind::index: 
2913     value = detail::get_dynamic_spec<Handler>(ctx.arg(ref.val.index), 
2914                                               ctx.error_handler()); 
2915     break; 
2916   case arg_id_kind::name: 
2917     value = detail::get_dynamic_spec<Handler>(ctx.arg(ref.val.name), 
2918                                               ctx.error_handler()); 
2919     break; 
2920   } 
2921 }

2923 using format_func = void (*)(detail::buffer<char>&, int, string_view); 
2924  
2925 FMT_API void format_error_code(buffer<char>& out, int error_code, 
2926                                string_view message) FMT_NOEXCEPT; 
2927  
2928 FMT_API void report_error(format_func func, int error_code, 
2929                           string_view message) FMT_NOEXCEPT;

2931 /** The default argument formatter. */ 
2932 template <typename OutputIt, typename Char> 
2933 class arg_formatter : public arg_formatter_base<OutputIt, Char> { 
2934  private: 
2935   using char_type = Char; 
2936   using base = arg_formatter_base<OutputIt, Char>; 
2937   using context_type = basic_format_context<OutputIt, Char>; 
2938  
2939   context_type& ctx_; 
2940   basic_format_parse_context<char_type>* parse_ctx_; 
2941   const Char* ptr_; 
2942  
2943  public: 
2944   using iterator = typename base::iterator; 
2945   using format_specs = typename base::format_specs; 
2946  
2947   /** 
2948     \rst 
2949     Constructs an argument formatter object. 
2950     *ctx* is a reference to the formatting context, 
2951     *specs* contains format specifier information for standard argument types. 
2952     \endrst 
2953    */ 
2954   explicit arg_formatter( 
2955       context_type& ctx, 
2956       basic_format_parse_context<char_type>* parse_ctx = nullptr, 
2957       format_specs* specs = nullptr, const Char* ptr = nullptr) 
2958       : base(ctx.out(), specs, ctx.locale()), 
2959         ctx_(ctx), 
2960         parse_ctx_(parse_ctx), 
2961         ptr_(ptr) {} 
2962  
2963   using base::operator(); 
2964  
2965   /** Formats an argument of a user-defined type. */ 
2966   iterator operator()(typename basic_format_arg<context_type>::handle handle) { 
2967     if (ptr_) advance_to(*parse_ctx_, ptr_); 
2968     handle.format(*parse_ctx_, ctx_); 
2969     return ctx_.out(); 
2970   } 
2971 }; 
2972 }  // namespace detail

2976     detail::arg_formatter<OutputIt, Char>;
