2978 /** 
2979  An error returned by an operating system or a language runtime, 
2980  for example a file opening error. 
2981 */ 
2982 FMT_CLASS_API 
2983 class FMT_API system_error : public std::runtime_error { 
2984  private: 
2985   void init(int err_code, string_view format_str, format_args args); 
2986  
2987  protected: 
2988   int error_code_; 
2989  
2990   system_error() : std::runtime_error(""), error_code_(0) {} 
2991  
2992  public: 
2993   /** 
2994    \rst 
2995    Constructs a :class:`fmt::system_error` object with a description 
2996    formatted with `fmt::format_system_error`. *message* and additional 
2997    arguments passed into the constructor are formatted similarly to 
2998    `fmt::format`. 
2999  
3000    **Example**:: 
3001  
3002      // This throws a system_error with the description 
3003      //   cannot open file 'madeup': No such file or directory 
3004      // or similar (system message may vary). 
3005      const char *filename = "madeup"; 
3006      std::FILE *file = std::fopen(filename, "r"); 
3007      if (!file) 
3008        throw fmt::system_error(errno, "cannot open file '{}'", filename); 
3009    \endrst 
3010   */ 
3011   template <typename... Args> 
3012   system_error(int error_code, string_view message, const Args&... args) 
3013       : std::runtime_error("") { 
3014     init(error_code, message, make_format_args(args...)); 
3015   } 
3016   system_error(const system_error&) = default; 
3017   system_error& operator=(const system_error&) = default; 
3018   system_error(system_error&&) = default; 
3019   system_error& operator=(system_error&&) = default; 
3020   ~system_error() FMT_NOEXCEPT FMT_OVERRIDE; 
3021  
3022   int error_code() const { return error_code_; } 
3023 }; 
3024  
3025 /** 
3026   \rst 
3027   Formats an error returned by an operating system or a language runtime, 
3028   for example a file opening error, and writes it to *out* in the following 
3029   form: 
3030  
3031   .. parsed-literal:: 
3032      *<message>*: *<system-message>* 
3033  
3034   where *<message>* is the passed message and *<system-message>* is 
3035   the system message corresponding to the error code. 
3036   *error_code* is a system error code as given by ``errno``. 
3037   If *error_code* is not a valid error code such as -1, the system message 
3038   may look like "Unknown error -1" and is platform-dependent. 
3039   \endrst 
3040  */ 
3041 FMT_API void format_system_error(detail::buffer<char>& out, int error_code, 
3042                                  string_view message) FMT_NOEXCEPT; 
3043  
3044 // Reports a system error without throwing an exception. 
3045 // Can be used to report errors from destructors. 
3046 FMT_API void report_system_error(int error_code, 
3047                                  string_view message) FMT_NOEXCEPT; 
3048  
3049 /** Fast integer formatter. */ 
3050 class format_int { 
3051  private: 
3052   // Buffer should be large enough to hold all digits (digits10 + 1), 
3053   // a sign and a null character. 
3054   enum { buffer_size = std::numeric_limits<unsigned long long>::digits10 + 3 }; 
3055   mutable char buffer_[buffer_size]; 
3056   char* str_; 
3057  
3058   template <typename UInt> char* format_unsigned(UInt value) { 
3059     auto n = static_cast<detail::uint32_or_64_or_128_t<UInt>>(value); 
3060     return detail::format_decimal(buffer_, n, buffer_size - 1).begin; 
3061   } 
3062  
3063   template <typename Int> char* format_signed(Int value) { 
3064     auto abs_value = static_cast<detail::uint32_or_64_or_128_t<Int>>(value); 
3065     bool negative = value < 0; 
3066     if (negative) abs_value = 0 - abs_value; 
3067     auto begin = format_unsigned(abs_value); 
3068     if (negative) *--begin = '-'; 
3069     return begin; 
3070   } 
3071  
3072  public: 
3073   explicit format_int(int value) : str_(format_signed(value)) {} 
3074   explicit format_int(long value) : str_(format_signed(value)) {} 
3075   explicit format_int(long long value) : str_(format_signed(value)) {} 
3076   explicit format_int(unsigned value) : str_(format_unsigned(value)) {} 
3077   explicit format_int(unsigned long value) : str_(format_unsigned(value)) {} 
3078   explicit format_int(unsigned long long value) 
3079       : str_(format_unsigned(value)) {} 
3080  
3081   /** Returns the number of characters written to the output buffer. */ 
3082   size_t size() const { 
3083     return detail::to_unsigned(buffer_ - str_ + buffer_size - 1); 
3084   } 
3085  
3086   /** 
3087     Returns a pointer to the output buffer content. No terminating null 
3088     character is appended. 
3089    */ 
3090   const char* data() const { return str_; } 
3091  
3092   /** 
3093     Returns a pointer to the output buffer content with terminating null 
3094     character appended. 
3095    */ 
3096   const char* c_str() const { 
3097     buffer_[buffer_size - 1] = '\0'; 
3098     return str_; 
3099   } 
3100  
3101   /** 
3102     \rst 
3103     Returns the content of the output buffer as an ``std::string``. 
3104     \endrst 
3105    */ 
3106   std::string str() const { return std::string(str_, size()); } 
3107 }; 
3108  
3109 // A formatter specialization for the core types corresponding to detail::type 
3110 // constants. 
3111 template <typename T, typename Char> 
3112 struct formatter<T, Char, 
3113                  enable_if_t<detail::type_constant<T, Char>::value != 
3114                              detail::type::custom_type>> { 
3115   FMT_CONSTEXPR formatter() = default; 
3116  
3117   // Parses format specifiers stopping either at the end of the range or at the 
3118   // terminating '}'. 
3119   template <typename ParseContext> 
3120   FMT_CONSTEXPR auto parse(ParseContext& ctx) -> decltype(ctx.begin()) { 
3121     using handler_type = detail::dynamic_specs_handler<ParseContext>; 
3122     auto type = detail::type_constant<T, Char>::value; 
3123     detail::specs_checker<handler_type> handler(handler_type(specs_, ctx), 
3124                                                 type); 
3125     auto it = parse_format_specs(ctx.begin(), ctx.end(), handler); 
3126     auto eh = ctx.error_handler(); 
3127     switch (type) { 
3128     case detail::type::none_type: 
3129       FMT_ASSERT(false, "invalid argument type"); 
3130       break; 
3131     case detail::type::int_type: 
3132     case detail::type::uint_type: 
3133     case detail::type::long_long_type: 
3134     case detail::type::ulong_long_type: 
3135     case detail::type::int128_type: 
3136     case detail::type::uint128_type: 
3137     case detail::type::bool_type: 
3138       handle_int_type_spec(specs_.type, 
3139                            detail::int_type_checker<decltype(eh)>(eh)); 
3140       break; 
3141     case detail::type::char_type: 
3142       handle_char_specs( 
3143           &specs_, detail::char_specs_checker<decltype(eh)>(specs_.type, eh)); 
3144       break; 
3145     case detail::type::float_type: 
3146       if (detail::const_check(FMT_USE_FLOAT)) 
3147         detail::parse_float_type_spec(specs_, eh); 
3148       else 
3149         FMT_ASSERT(false, "float support disabled"); 
3150       break; 
3151     case detail::type::double_type: 
3152       if (detail::const_check(FMT_USE_DOUBLE)) 
3153         detail::parse_float_type_spec(specs_, eh); 
3154       else 
3155         FMT_ASSERT(false, "double support disabled"); 
3156       break; 
3157     case detail::type::long_double_type: 
3158       if (detail::const_check(FMT_USE_LONG_DOUBLE)) 
3159         detail::parse_float_type_spec(specs_, eh); 
3160       else 
3161         FMT_ASSERT(false, "long double support disabled"); 
3162       break; 
3163     case detail::type::cstring_type: 
3164       detail::handle_cstring_type_spec( 
3165           specs_.type, detail::cstring_type_checker<decltype(eh)>(eh)); 
3166       break; 
3167     case detail::type::string_type: 
3168       detail::check_string_type_spec(specs_.type, eh); 
3169       break; 
3170     case detail::type::pointer_type: 
3171       detail::check_pointer_type_spec(specs_.type, eh); 
3172       break; 
3173     case detail::type::custom_type: 
3174       // Custom format specifiers should be checked in parse functions of 
3175       // formatter specializations. 
3176       break; 
3177     } 
3178     return it; 
3179   } 
3180  
3181   template <typename FormatContext> 
3182   auto format(const T& val, FormatContext& ctx) -> decltype(ctx.out()) { 
3183     detail::handle_dynamic_spec<detail::width_checker>(specs_.width, 
3184                                                        specs_.width_ref, ctx); 
3185     detail::handle_dynamic_spec<detail::precision_checker>( 
3186         specs_.precision, specs_.precision_ref, ctx); 
3187     using af = detail::arg_formatter<typename FormatContext::iterator, 
3188                                      typename FormatContext::char_type>; 
3189     return visit_format_arg(af(ctx, nullptr, &specs_), 
3190                             detail::make_arg<FormatContext>(val)); 
3191   } 
3192  
3193  private: 
3194   detail::dynamic_format_specs<Char> specs_; 
3195 }; 
3196  
3197 #define FMT_FORMAT_AS(Type, Base)                                             \ 
3198   template <typename Char>                                                    \ 
3199   struct formatter<Type, Char> : formatter<Base, Char> {                      \ 
3200     template <typename FormatContext>                                         \ 
3201     auto format(Type const& val, FormatContext& ctx) -> decltype(ctx.out()) { \ 
3202       return formatter<Base, Char>::format(val, ctx);                         \ 
3203     }                                                                         \ 
3204   } 
3205  
3206 FMT_FORMAT_AS(signed char, int); 
3207 FMT_FORMAT_AS(unsigned char, unsigned); 
3208 FMT_FORMAT_AS(short, int); 
3209 FMT_FORMAT_AS(unsigned short, unsigned); 
3210 FMT_FORMAT_AS(long, long long); 
3211 FMT_FORMAT_AS(unsigned long, unsigned long long); 
3212 FMT_FORMAT_AS(Char*, const Char*); 
3213 FMT_FORMAT_AS(std::basic_string<Char>, basic_string_view<Char>); 
3214 FMT_FORMAT_AS(std::nullptr_t, const void*); 
3215 FMT_FORMAT_AS(detail::std_string_view<Char>, basic_string_view<Char>); 
3216  
3217 template <typename Char> 
3218 struct formatter<void*, Char> : formatter<const void*, Char> { 
3219   template <typename FormatContext> 
3220   auto format(void* val, FormatContext& ctx) -> decltype(ctx.out()) { 
3221     return formatter<const void*, Char>::format(val, ctx); 
3222   }

3225 template <typename Char, size_t N> 
3226 struct formatter<Char[N], Char> : formatter<basic_string_view<Char>, Char> { 
3227   template <typename FormatContext> 
3228   auto format(const Char* val, FormatContext& ctx) -> decltype(ctx.out()) { 
3229     return formatter<basic_string_view<Char>, Char>::format(val, ctx);

3232  
3233 // A formatter for types known only at run time such as variant alternatives. 
3234 // 
3235 // Usage: 
3236 //   using variant = std::variant<int, std::string>; 
3237 //   template <> 
3238 //   struct formatter<variant>: dynamic_formatter<> { 
3239 //     void format(buffer &buf, const variant &v, context &ctx) { 
3240 //       visit([&](const auto &val) { format(buf, val, ctx); }, v); 
3241 //     } 
3242 //   }; 
3243 template <typename Char = char> class dynamic_formatter { 
3244  private: 
3245   struct null_handler : detail::error_handler { 
3246     void on_align(align_t) {} 
3247     void on_plus() {} 
3248     void on_minus() {} 
3249     void on_space() {} 
3250     void on_hash() {} 
3251   }; 
3252  
3253  public: 
3254   template <typename ParseContext> 
3255   auto parse(ParseContext& ctx) -> decltype(ctx.begin()) { 
3256     format_str_ = ctx.begin(); 
3257     // Checks are deferred to formatting time when the argument type is known. 
3258     detail::dynamic_specs_handler<ParseContext> handler(specs_, ctx); 
3259     return parse_format_specs(ctx.begin(), ctx.end(), handler); 
3260   } 
3261  
3262   template <typename T, typename FormatContext> 
3263   auto format(const T& val, FormatContext& ctx) -> decltype(ctx.out()) { 
3264     handle_specs(ctx); 
3265     detail::specs_checker<null_handler> checker( 
3266         null_handler(), detail::mapped_type_constant<T, FormatContext>::value); 
3267     checker.on_align(specs_.align); 
3268     switch (specs_.sign) { 
3269     case sign::none: 
3270       break; 
3271     case sign::plus: 
3272       checker.on_plus(); 
3273       break; 
3274     case sign::minus: 
3275       checker.on_minus(); 
3276       break; 
3277     case sign::space: 
3278       checker.on_space(); 
3279       break; 
3280     } 
3281     if (specs_.alt) checker.on_hash(); 
3282     if (specs_.precision >= 0) checker.end_precision(); 
3283     using af = detail::arg_formatter<typename FormatContext::iterator, 
3284                                      typename FormatContext::char_type>; 
3285     visit_format_arg(af(ctx, nullptr, &specs_), 
3286                      detail::make_arg<FormatContext>(val)); 
3287     return ctx.out(); 
3288   } 
3289  
3290  private: 
3291   template <typename Context> void handle_specs(Context& ctx) { 
3292     detail::handle_dynamic_spec<detail::width_checker>(specs_.width, 
3293                                                        specs_.width_ref, ctx); 
3294     detail::handle_dynamic_spec<detail::precision_checker>( 
3295         specs_.precision, specs_.precision_ref, ctx); 
3296   } 
3297  
3298   detail::dynamic_format_specs<Char> specs_; 
3299   const Char* format_str_; 
3300 }; 
3301  
3302 template <typename Char, typename ErrorHandler> 
3303 FMT_CONSTEXPR void advance_to( 
3304     basic_format_parse_context<Char, ErrorHandler>& ctx, const Char* p) { 
3305   ctx.advance_to(ctx.begin() + (p - &*ctx.begin())); 
3306 } 
3307  
3308 /** Formats arguments and writes the output to the range. */ 
3309 template <typename ArgFormatter, typename Char, typename Context> 
3310 typename Context::iterator vformat_to( 
3311     typename ArgFormatter::iterator out, basic_string_view<Char> format_str, 
3312     basic_format_args<Context> args, 
3313     detail::locale_ref loc = detail::locale_ref()) { 
3314   if (format_str.size() == 2 && detail::equal2(format_str.data(), "{}")) { 
3315     auto arg = args.get(0); 
3316     if (!arg) detail::error_handler().on_error("argument not found"); 
3317     using iterator = typename ArgFormatter::iterator; 
3318     return visit_format_arg( 
3319         detail::default_arg_formatter<iterator, Char>{out, args, loc}, arg); 
3320   } 
3321   detail::format_handler<ArgFormatter, Char, Context> h(out, format_str, args, 
3322                                                         loc); 
3323   detail::parse_format_string<false>(format_str, h); 
3324   return h.context.out(); 
3325 }

3327 // Casts ``p`` to ``const void*`` for pointer formatting. 
3328 // Example: 
3329 //   auto s = format("{}", ptr(p)); 
3330 template <typename T> inline const void* ptr(const T* p) { return p; } 
3331 template <typename T> inline const void* ptr(const std::unique_ptr<T>& p) { 
3332   return p.get(); 
3333 } 
3334 template <typename T> inline const void* ptr(const std::shared_ptr<T>& p) { 
3335   return p.get(); 
3336 }

3338 class bytes { 
3339  private:

3342  
3343  public: 
3344   explicit bytes(string_view data) : data_(data) {} 
3345 };

3347 template <> struct formatter<bytes> { 
3348  private: 
3349   detail::dynamic_format_specs<char> specs_; 
3350  
3351  public: 
3352   template <typename ParseContext> 
3353   FMT_CONSTEXPR auto parse(ParseContext& ctx) -> decltype(ctx.begin()) { 
3354     using handler_type = detail::dynamic_specs_handler<ParseContext>; 
3355     detail::specs_checker<handler_type> handler(handler_type(specs_, ctx), 
3356                                                 detail::type::string_type); 
3357     auto it = parse_format_specs(ctx.begin(), ctx.end(), handler); 
3358     detail::check_string_type_spec(specs_.type, ctx.error_handler()); 
3359     return it; 
3360   } 
3361  
3362   template <typename FormatContext> 
3363   auto format(bytes b, FormatContext& ctx) -> decltype(ctx.out()) { 
3364     detail::handle_dynamic_spec<detail::width_checker>(specs_.width, 
3365                                                        specs_.width_ref, ctx); 
3366     detail::handle_dynamic_spec<detail::precision_checker>( 
3367         specs_.precision, specs_.precision_ref, ctx); 
3368     return detail::write_bytes(ctx.out(), b.data_, specs_); 
3369   }

3372 template <typename It, typename Sentinel, typename Char> 
3373 struct arg_join : detail::view { 
3374   It begin; 
3375   Sentinel end; 
3376   basic_string_view<Char> sep; 
3377  
3378   arg_join(It b, Sentinel e, basic_string_view<Char> s) 
3379       : begin(b), end(e), sep(s) {} 
3380 }; 
3381  
3382 template <typename It, typename Sentinel, typename Char> 
3383 struct formatter<arg_join<It, Sentinel, Char>, Char> 
3384     : formatter<typename std::iterator_traits<It>::value_type, Char> { 
3385   template <typename FormatContext> 
3386   auto format(const arg_join<It, Sentinel, Char>& value, FormatContext& ctx) 
3387       -> decltype(ctx.out()) { 
3388     using base = formatter<typename std::iterator_traits<It>::value_type, Char>; 
3389     auto it = value.begin; 
3390     auto out = ctx.out(); 
3391     if (it != value.end) { 
3392       out = base::format(*it++, ctx); 
3393       while (it != value.end) { 
3394         out = std::copy(value.sep.begin(), value.sep.end(), out); 
3395         ctx.advance_to(out); 
3396         out = base::format(*it++, ctx); 
3397       } 
3398     } 
3399     return out; 
3400   } 
3401 };

3403 /** 
3404   Returns an object that formats the iterator range `[begin, end)` with elements 
3405   separated by `sep`. 
3406  */ 
3407 template <typename It, typename Sentinel> 
3408 arg_join<It, Sentinel, char> join(It begin, Sentinel end, string_view sep) { 
3409   return {begin, end, sep}; 
3410 }

3412 template <typename It, typename Sentinel> 
3413 arg_join<It, Sentinel, wchar_t> join(It begin, Sentinel end, wstring_view sep) { 
3414   return {begin, end, sep};

3416  
3417 /** 
3418   \rst 
3419   Returns an object that formats `range` with elements separated by `sep`. 
3420  
3421   **Example**:: 
3422  
3423     std::vector<int> v = {1, 2, 3}; 
3424     fmt::print("{}", fmt::join(v, ", ")); 
3425     // Output: "1, 2, 3" 
3426  
3427   ``fmt::join`` applies passed format specifiers to the range elements:: 
3428  
3429     fmt::print("{:02}", fmt::join(v, ", ")); 
3430     // Output: "01, 02, 03" 
3431   \endrst 
3432  */ 
3433 template <typename Range> 
3434 arg_join<detail::iterator_t<Range>, detail::sentinel_t<Range>, char> join( 
3435     Range&& range, string_view sep) { 
3436   return join(std::begin(range), std::end(range), sep); 
3437 } 
3438  
3439 template <typename Range> 
3440 arg_join<detail::iterator_t<Range>, detail::sentinel_t<Range>, wchar_t> join( 
3441     Range&& range, wstring_view sep) { 
3442   return join(std::begin(range), std::end(range), sep); 
3443 }

3445 /** 
3446   \rst 
3447   Converts *value* to ``std::string`` using the default format for type *T*. 
3448  
3449   **Example**:: 
3450  
3451     #include <fmt/format.h> 
3452  
3453     std::string answer = fmt::to_string(42); 
3454   \endrst 
3455  */ 
3456 template <typename T, FMT_ENABLE_IF(!std::is_integral<T>::value)> 
3457 inline std::string to_string(const T& value) { 
3458   std::string result; 
3459   detail::write<char>(std::back_inserter(result), value); 
3460   return result; 
3461 } 
3462  
3463 template <typename T, FMT_ENABLE_IF(std::is_integral<T>::value)> 
3464 inline std::string to_string(T value) { 
3465   // The buffer should be large enough to store the number including the sign or 
3466   // "false" for bool. 
3467   constexpr int max_size = detail::digits10<T>() + 2; 
3468   char buffer[max_size > 5 ? max_size : 5]; 
3469   char* begin = buffer; 
3470   return std::string(begin, detail::write<char>(begin, value)); 
3471 } 
3472  
3473 /** 
3474   Converts *value* to ``std::wstring`` using the default format for type *T*. 
3475  */ 
3476 template <typename T> inline std::wstring to_wstring(const T& value) { 
3477   return format(L"{}", value); 
3478 } 
3479  
3480 template <typename Char, size_t SIZE> 
3481 std::basic_string<Char> to_string(const basic_memory_buffer<Char, SIZE>& buf) { 
3482   auto size = buf.size(); 
3483   detail::assume(size < std::basic_string<Char>().max_size()); 
3484   return std::basic_string<Char>(buf.data(), size); 
3485 } 
3486  
3487 template <typename Char> 
3488 detail::buffer_appender<Char> detail::vformat_to( 
3489     detail::buffer<Char>& buf, basic_string_view<Char> format_str, 
3490     basic_format_args<buffer_context<type_identity_t<Char>>> args) { 
3491   using af = arg_formatter<typename buffer_context<Char>::iterator, Char>; 
3492   return vformat_to<af>(buffer_appender<Char>(buf), format_str, args); 
3493 } 
3494  
3495 #ifndef FMT_HEADER_ONLY 
3496 extern template format_context::iterator detail::vformat_to( 
3497     detail::buffer<char>&, string_view, basic_format_args<format_context>); 
3498 namespace detail { 
3499 extern template FMT_API std::string grouping_impl<char>(locale_ref loc); 
3500 extern template FMT_API std::string grouping_impl<wchar_t>(locale_ref loc); 
3501 extern template FMT_API char thousands_sep_impl<char>(locale_ref loc); 
3502 extern template FMT_API wchar_t thousands_sep_impl<wchar_t>(locale_ref loc); 
3503 extern template FMT_API char decimal_point_impl(locale_ref loc); 
3504 extern template FMT_API wchar_t decimal_point_impl(locale_ref loc); 
3505 extern template int format_float<double>(double value, int precision, 
3506                                          float_specs specs, buffer<char>& buf); 
3507 extern template int format_float<long double>(long double value, int precision, 
3508                                               float_specs specs, 
3509                                               buffer<char>& buf); 
3510 int snprintf_float(float value, int precision, float_specs specs, 
3511                    buffer<char>& buf) = delete; 
3512 extern template int snprintf_float<double>(double value, int precision, 
3513                                            float_specs specs, 
3514                                            buffer<char>& buf); 
3515 extern template int snprintf_float<long double>(long double value, 
3516                                                 int precision, 
3517                                                 float_specs specs, 
3518                                                 buffer<char>& buf); 
3519 }  // namespace detail 
3520 #endif 
3521  
3522 template <typename S, typename Char = char_t<S>, 
3523           FMT_ENABLE_IF(detail::is_string<S>::value)> 
3524 inline typename FMT_BUFFER_CONTEXT(Char)::iterator vformat_to( 
3525     detail::buffer<Char>& buf, const S& format_str, 
3526     basic_format_args<FMT_BUFFER_CONTEXT(type_identity_t<Char>)> args) { 
3527   return detail::vformat_to(buf, to_string_view(format_str), args); 
3528 } 
3529  
3530 template <typename S, typename... Args, size_t SIZE = inline_buffer_size, 
3531           typename Char = enable_if_t<detail::is_string<S>::value, char_t<S>>> 
3532 inline typename buffer_context<Char>::iterator format_to( 
3533     basic_memory_buffer<Char, SIZE>& buf, const S& format_str, Args&&... args) { 
3534   const auto& vargs = fmt::make_args_checked<Args...>(format_str, args...); 
3535   return detail::vformat_to(buf, to_string_view(format_str), vargs); 
3536 } 
3537  
3538 template <typename OutputIt, typename Char = char> 
3539 using format_context_t = basic_format_context<OutputIt, Char>; 
3540  
3541 template <typename OutputIt, typename Char = char> 
3542 using format_args_t = basic_format_args<format_context_t<OutputIt, Char>>; 
3543  
3544 template <typename OutputIt, typename Char = typename OutputIt::value_type> 
3545 using format_to_n_context FMT_DEPRECATED_ALIAS = buffer_context<Char>; 
3546  
3547 template <typename OutputIt, typename Char = typename OutputIt::value_type> 
3548 using format_to_n_args FMT_DEPRECATED_ALIAS = 
3549     basic_format_args<buffer_context<Char>>; 
3550  
3551 template <typename OutputIt, typename Char, typename... Args> 
3552 FMT_DEPRECATED format_arg_store<buffer_context<Char>, Args...> 
3553 make_format_to_n_args(const Args&... args) { 
3554   return format_arg_store<buffer_context<Char>, Args...>(args...); 
3555 } 
3556  
3557 template <typename Char, enable_if_t<(!std::is_same<Char, char>::value), int>> 
3558 std::basic_string<Char> detail::vformat( 
3559     basic_string_view<Char> format_str, 
3560     basic_format_args<buffer_context<type_identity_t<Char>>> args) { 
3561   basic_memory_buffer<Char> buffer; 
3562   detail::vformat_to(buffer, format_str, args); 
3563   return to_string(buffer); 
3564 } 
3565  
3566 template <typename Char, FMT_ENABLE_IF(std::is_same<Char, wchar_t>::value)> 
3567 void vprint(std::FILE* f, basic_string_view<Char> format_str, 
3568             wformat_args args) { 
3569   wmemory_buffer buffer; 
3570   detail::vformat_to(buffer, format_str, args); 
3571   buffer.push_back(L'\0'); 
3572   if (std::fputws(buffer.data(), f) == -1) 
3573     FMT_THROW(system_error(errno, "cannot write to file")); 
3574 } 
3575  
3576 template <typename Char, FMT_ENABLE_IF(std::is_same<Char, wchar_t>::value)> 
3577 void vprint(basic_string_view<Char> format_str, wformat_args args) { 
3578   vprint(stdout, format_str, args); 
3579 }

3581 #if FMT_USE_USER_DEFINED_LITERALS 
3582 namespace detail { 
3583  
3584 #  if FMT_USE_UDL_TEMPLATE 
3585 template <typename Char, Char... CHARS> class udl_formatter { 
3586  public: 
3587   template <typename... Args> 
3588   std::basic_string<Char> operator()(Args&&... args) const { 
3589     static FMT_CONSTEXPR_DECL Char s[] = {CHARS..., '\0'}; 
3590     return format(FMT_STRING(s), std::forward<Args>(args)...); 
3591   } 
3592 }; 
3593 #  else 
3594 template <typename Char> struct udl_formatter { 
3595   basic_string_view<Char> str; 
3596  
3597   template <typename... Args> 
3598   std::basic_string<Char> operator()(Args&&... args) const { 
3599     return format(str, std::forward<Args>(args)...); 
3600   } 
3601 }; 
3602 #  endif  // FMT_USE_UDL_TEMPLATE 
3603  
3604 template <typename Char> struct udl_arg { 
3605   const Char* str; 
3606  
3607   template <typename T> named_arg<Char, T> operator=(T&& value) const { 
3608     return {str, std::forward<T>(value)}; 
3609   } 
3610 }; 
3611 }  // namespace detail 
3612  
3613 inline namespace literals { 
3614 #  if FMT_USE_UDL_TEMPLATE 
3615 #    pragma GCC diagnostic push 
3616 #    pragma GCC diagnostic ignored "-Wpedantic" 
3617 #    if FMT_CLANG_VERSION 
3618 #      pragma GCC diagnostic ignored "-Wgnu-string-literal-operator-template" 
3619 #    endif 
3620 template <typename Char, Char... CHARS> 
3621 FMT_CONSTEXPR detail::udl_formatter<Char, CHARS...> operator""_format() { 
3622   return {}; 
3623 } 
3624 #    pragma GCC diagnostic pop 
3625 #  else 
3626 /** 
3627   \rst 
3628   User-defined literal equivalent of :func:`fmt::format`. 
3629  
3630   **Example**:: 
3631  
3632     using namespace fmt::literals; 
3633     std::string message = "The answer is {}"_format(42); 
3634   \endrst 
3635  */ 
3636 FMT_CONSTEXPR detail::udl_formatter<char> operator"" _format(const char* s, 
3637                                                              size_t n) { 
3638   return {{s, n}}; 
3639 } 
3640 FMT_CONSTEXPR detail::udl_formatter<wchar_t> operator"" _format( 
3641     const wchar_t* s, size_t n) { 
3642   return {{s, n}}; 
3643 } 
3644 #  endif  // FMT_USE_UDL_TEMPLATE 
3645  
3646 /** 
3647   \rst 
3648   User-defined literal equivalent of :func:`fmt::arg`. 
3649  
3650   **Example**:: 
3651  
3652     using namespace fmt::literals; 
3653     fmt::print("Elapsed time: {s:.2f} seconds", "s"_a=1.23); 
3654   \endrst 
3655  */ 
3656 FMT_CONSTEXPR detail::udl_arg<char> operator"" _a(const char* s, size_t) { 
3657   return {s}; 
3658 } 
3659 FMT_CONSTEXPR detail::udl_arg<wchar_t> operator"" _a(const wchar_t* s, size_t) { 
3660   return {s}; 
3661 } 
3662 }  // namespace literals 
3663 #endif  // FMT_USE_USER_DEFINED_LITERALS
